## Executive dashboard

**What we are building.** Give the tool a song and get back a preset for the Line 6 HX Stomp guitar pedal that sounds like the guitar on the recording, checked on the real pedal. It works by trying candidate presets, comparing the sound with the recording, and adjusting until they match.

**Project Health:** ▲ At Risk  
Reason: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3, run once on the fresh sealed set of 96 known-answer items, met all seven checks of the frozen rule: category 0.885 (bar 0.85), model 0.771 (bar 0.60), settings error 0.101 (bar 0.15), ranking the true preset first 0.979 (bar 0.95), better than the random walk on model (+0.208, interval low end +0.094) and on settings (-0.083, whole interval below zero), and the consistency split, which carries no information on this set. The ranking check was amended by the human before the run so that two settings differing only in volume count as a tie; attempts 1 and 2 keep their figures under the old rule. The gate is not passed until the human signs. At Risk rather than On Track: pedal results still cannot be counted, real songs are unmeasured, the test is the known-clean-recording case only, and delay model accuracy is weak (5 of 12).

**Technical Progress:** Advancing  
Reason: Engineering advanced a long way on 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18: specialists for each effect family, a crash-safe parallel job runner that scales to the machine's core count, a portable load measure, a level-aware tie rule for the ranking check, a gain-aware score term, and a mod-family prior. Attempt 3 moved category to 0.885 and model to 0.771 and met every check. Advancing is earned on the known-answer test only; real songs and the pedal are not yet advanced.

**Product Readiness:** Prototype (stage 2 of 5)  
Reason: Important pieces are demonstrated: a real song was matched by a script and listened to, the tone comparison passes on single blocks with a known clean recording, and the known-answer run now meets every [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") check (category 88.5 percent, model 77 percent of 96 sealed items, settings close). [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") is not signed yet, so the system cannot be called Validated, and the pieces are not shown to work together from a song to a pedal-checked preset.  
Blocking the next stage: Integrated needs one command from song to a pedal-checked preset, a chain builder that has run on a real engine, and a comparison that works without a lined-up clean recording. Validated also needs the human's [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") sign-off.

Readiness stages (the highest demonstrated is in bold): Foundation → **Prototype** → Integrated → Validated → Ready

**Summary:** The goal is a tool that listens to a recorded guitar tone and builds a matching preset for the HX Stomp pedal. The third attempt at [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), the known-answer test on 96 single effects, met all seven agreed checks: the tool names the effect category 88.5 percent of the time and the exact model 77 percent, finds close settings, beats a random-guess baseline, and its comparison score ranks the true preset first on 94 of 96 items. The gate is not passed until the owner signs it. The ranking check was relaxed by the owner beforehand so that settings differing only in loudness count as a tie, and this is the known-clean-recording case only. The weakest effect is now delay (5 of 12 exact models, mostly one delay mistaken for another). Nothing yet turns a song into a finished preset in one step.

> **Biggest problem: Delay is the weakest effect: 5 of 12 exact models on the sealed set** (High, Confirmed Problem)
>
> **What is wrong.** On [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 (96 sealed items, all seven checks met) the exact model was right on cab 12, eq 11, reverb 11, mod 10, comp 10, amp 8, drive 7 and delay 5 of 12. The seven delay misses: four named another delay model (i001, i049, i073, i089), three a different kind of effect (i041 a compressor, i065 and i081 an equaliser). Two ranking misses (i039, i095) are cabinets.  
> **Why it matters.** With [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") awaiting the owner's sign-off, the weakest measured part of the method is delay: the exact model was named on only 5 of 12 delay items on the sealed set (drive 7, amp 8; cabinet 12). Four of the seven misses name a different delay model, which may still sound close, and three name a different kind of effect. It is not a gate issue, but delay is common in real tones, so it is the next target for the search and the next thing to listen to (the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") page has the delay misses with the target on the left channel and our guess on the right).  
> **What it prevents.** Does not block [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), which met every check, but delay is common in real tones and the model is wrong on more than half of the delay items. Real songs will be harder than this known-clean-recording test.  
> **What resolves it.** Diagnose the delay misses on development data (echo time, feedback and mix versus model), then strengthen the time-based specialist; listen to the delay misses to see whether the swaps matter by ear.  
> **Next step.** Listen to the delay misses on the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") page, then decide the order of work.

**Next milestone: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)")** (At Risk). Show the tool can recover a known answer: given audio made from a preset we already know, its comparison measure picks the true preset and its search finds the right blocks and settings, at thresholds agreed in advance.  
Readiness: 3 of 3 acceptance criteria evidenced; owner sign-off pending.  
What must happen: The owner's sign-off. Later work (not a gate condition): delay model accuracy, drive, and real songs.  
Blocked by: Awaiting the owner's sign-off (2026-10-18). No check is failing.  
Next action: Owner: sign [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") or say what is missing. The gate ledger row is written only with the sign-off.

### Delay is the weakest effect: 5 of 12 exact models on the sealed set

**High** · Confirmed Problem

- **Problem.** On [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 (96 sealed items, all seven checks met) the exact model was right on cab 12, eq 11, reverb 11, mod 10, comp 10, amp 8, drive 7 and delay 5 of 12. The seven delay misses: four named another delay model (i001, i049, i073, i089), three a different kind of effect (i041 a compressor, i065 and i081 an equaliser). Two ranking misses (i039, i095) are cabinets.
- **Impact.** Does not block [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), which met every check, but delay is common in real tones and the model is wrong on more than half of the delay items. Real songs will be harder than this known-clean-recording test.
- **Cause.** Not yet diagnosed. Swell-type and ducked delays look similar to other delays at the settings drawn; v3 is now seen, so diagnosis must use the development sets.
- **Resolution.** Diagnose the delay misses on development data (echo time, feedback and mix versus model), then strengthen the time-based specialist; listen to the delay misses to see whether the swaps matter by ear.
- **Owner.** search builds the delay work; the human listens; the lead schedules
- **Next step.** Listen to the delay misses on the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") page, then decide the order of work.
- **Evidence.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off; Decision log; Gate checklists

### No pedal measurement counts until the sampling defects are handled

**High** · Confirmed Problem

- **Problem.** The pedal verification found real defects: the audio stream over USB is not stable for about 0.7 s after it starts, amps carry sound over from the previous render, the pedal refuses four effect models in whole-preset writes, and some delay and reverb settings glide slowly when changed in place. Setting truth passed 20 of 40 items, repeatability 12 of 17. A longer pre-roll fixed the first problem, but a one-sample slip still happens about once in 90 renders. The human confirmed the pedal's blocks are editable after sessions.
- **Impact.** The pedal is the final judge of the preset. Until the slip is detected or re-taken, pedal numbers cannot be used as evidence, and earlier pedal agreement figures are understated in noise. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") (Native only) is not affected; [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") is.
- **Cause.** Known for each defect (stream start-up, amp state, device refusals, parameter glide). The remaining slip has no recorded cause.
- **Resolution.** Detect and re-take slipped renders, find why four models are refused, measure or avoid the parameter glide, then re-register the pedal rules (float32 read-back, an absolute -60 dB settle criterion, floors from rested renders) and re-run.
- **Owner.** stomp-engine; the lead schedules the re-run
- **Next step.** Detect or re-take slipped renders, then fix the refusals and the glide.
- **Evidence.** Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing); Pedal verification, last full run on the longer pre-roll: setting truth 20 of 40; sentinel tripped on a real one-sample slip; Decision log; Task board

### The tone comparison is only admitted when the clean recording is known

**High** · Risk

- **Problem.** The tool's comparison measure passed its registered test on single-block Helix sound (0.990 against a bar of 0.90), but only for the case where the clean guitar recording behind the target is known and lined up. The earlier tests of the song case, where no clean recording exists, failed (about 7 correct in 40 on different notes). The song case with a separation stand-in reached 0.948 on single blocks and is not yet admitted; drive effects are its weak category.
- **Impact.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") can be passed on this footing, but real songs have no lined-up clean recording, so [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") matching has no validated score yet. On the one real song tried, the top two candidates were nearly tied.
- **Cause.** Level-free spectral comparison works when the notes match; it reacts to which notes were played when they do not. Four of seven test misses were drive Output changes, which this measure cannot see by design (a level guard handles loudness).
- **Resolution.** Admit the song case separately once an aligned recording exists, add note-proof measures for the no-recording case (deferred option C), and re-test on real separated songs.
- **Owner.** tone-metrics; the lead decides admission
- **Next step.** Propose admission of the separated-song case with the drive weakness named; take note-proof measures into the [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") (blind single block) method choice after research [T158](../../board/TASKS.md#t158 "No-DI method survey (human 2026-10-18").
- **Evidence.** Tone-comparison admission: second Helix trust set, test split scored once (96 items, 19 look-alikes each); Tone-comparison trust check on real Helix renders (40 known chains, 2,400 renders); Informal listening to the first real-song matches; Decision log; [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95)

### Guitar separation quality on real songs is unknown

**High** · Unknown/Unverified

- **Problem.** Guitar separation has been scored only on artificial mixes: quality falls from 21.2 dB (guitar alone) to 7.3 dB with a second guitar in the mix. No real song has a quality score.
- **Impact.** If the isolated guitar on a real song contains drums, vocals or artefacts, the tone comparison will chase those instead of the guitar and [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") results will be unreliable.
- **Cause.** No real-song stress set exists, and the only separation model aimed at guitar has no stated licence so it was not used.
- **Resolution.** Run the separation stress test on a few real songs, locally, and record how accuracy falls as the mix gets denser.
- **Owner.** separation, after the owner supplies songs
- **Next step.** Owner chooses the first songs (one action covers this and the [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") song set).
- **Evidence.** Separation run on the real song (no quality score); Separation stress curve on a synthetic mix (five densities)

### Building whole chains from single blocks is not yet shown to work

**Medium** · Unknown/Unverified

- **Problem.** Single blocks are matched first and whole chains are built from analysis: the kinds of block present, ordered by common patterns. The pattern prior now rests on 2,068 curated presets and shows strong order rules. But the block-presence detector passed its test only for the cabinet and for whether a gain stage is present; for delay, reverb and modulation it missed its bars. The chain builder exists and is tested only with fake engines.
- **Impact.** [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") (real songs) needs whole chains. Presence is used only as a soft hint, so every time-based block must be confirmed by rendering with and without it.
- **Cause.** Behind an amp and cabinet, single-block detectors call the extra block a drive almost every time; comparing against the clean recording cannot isolate a block that follows a non-linear amp.
- **Resolution.** Judge post-amp blocks against the chain's current best render, render a multi-block test set on Native, and run the chain builder on a real engine. Cabinet choice by deconvolution looks promising.
- **Owner.** search (builder), tone-metrics (detector), native-engine (multi-block renders)
- **Next step.** Schedule the multi-block render set; this is [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") work and does not block [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)").
- **Evidence.** Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only); Preset-corpus chain statistics (category level only); Block-presence detector scored once on its test split (single Helix Native blocks); Chain builder design and fake-engine tests; Cabinet impulse-response library analysis (aggregate note, vendor-free); Decision log; Task board

#### The pedal and Native disagree on one cabinet setting

**High** · Confirmed Problem

- **Problem.** The cabinet's Early Reflections setting does not sound the same on the pedal and in Native.
- **Impact.** A preset found on the fast Native engine can sound different on the real pedal. At the value the search chose, the two engines' recordings cancel by only 3.6 dB (poor agreement); with the setting at 0 they cancel by 31 dB (close agreement).
- **Cause.** Not yet known. Candidates are an older algorithm in the pedal, a different parameter scale, or a delay-structure difference. Other parameters tested at their extremes agreed.
- **Resolution.** Find the cause, or measure every searchable parameter on both engines and let the search free only the parameters that agree.
- **Owner.** native-engine and stomp-engine; benchmark for the per-parameter audit
- **Next step.** Keep the setting at 0 for pedal presets and let the pedal check it (the accepted rule); the verification suites cover settings generally.
- **Evidence.** Pedal versus Native agreement on four presets; Decision log; Pedal versus Native agreement on firmware 3.80 (spot checks)

#### Native verification leaves recorded misses that are explained, not fixed

**Medium** · Confirmed Problem

- **Problem.** After three engine fixes (baked controls no longer sent, hosts that start in the other numeric mode are re-prepared, a state latch from the first amp loaded), repeatability is 216 of 216 and nothing unexplained remains. Two rules still miss as written: setting truth 116 of 120 and baked values 63 of 73. Seven misses are one-step float rounding in how Native lands a control value; seven are effect-loop and send blocks.
- **Impact.** None blocks [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") (ruled acceptable on 2026-10-17). Native results produced before the fixes were made by the earlier engine and may differ by rounding up to about -21 dB on a few chains.
- **Cause.** Native's own arithmetic and the nature of input and output blocks.
- **Resolution.** Exclude those blocks from search pools (done), and re-state the rule with a one-step tolerance for the next version.
- **Owner.** native-engine; the lead owns the rule wording
- **Next step.** None needed before the second [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt.
- **Evidence.** Native setting check, setting truth: 116 of 120 pairs, rule 2b 63 of 73; Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, baked values: zero violations; Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Native in-run sentinel catches a forced numeric-mode drift; Decision log

#### The software engine cannot stand in for every pedal effect

**Medium** · Risk

- **Problem.** Measurements show Native can stand in for the pedal on amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, most drives, delays, Room, Plate, Spring and Chamber reverbs, Hall above 500 Hz, and the modulation effects. It cannot for Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and the cabinet Early Reflections setting, and blocks that modulate on their own are left out of the search unless asked for.
- **Impact.** Presets that use those effects must be checked on the pedal, which is slower; the block library also has no measured fingerprints yet, so models are not yet chosen from measurements.
- **Cause.** The two engines run some effects differently, and Native's control steps limit fine settings (now 10 ms for delay time instead of 63 ms).
- **Resolution.** Keep the pedal as the checker for the listed effects; finish the block fingerprints and then model choice from the library.
- **Owner.** native-engine (fingerprints), search (model choice), benchmark (agreement measurements)
- **Next step.** Finish the block fingerprints (in progress), then model choice.
- **Evidence.** Native block library: 605 of 605 pedal models captured; Narrower control ranges honoured by Native (delay Time in 10 ms steps); Time-effect agreement measured with test tones (reverbs, modulation, delays); Gray Flanger and Deluxe Phaser agreement on 20 s tones; Pedal versus Native agreement on firmware 3.80 (spot checks)

#### Everything is verified on one computer, one pedal firmware, with a private helper

**Medium** · Risk

- **Problem.** Pedal control is verified on macOS and firmware 3.80 only, and the small helper program that talks to the pedal is intentionally not published.
- **Impact.** Other users on Windows or Linux may not be able to use pedal mode, and others cannot reproduce the pedal measurements.
- **Cause.** Only one machine is available, and the helper's source is withheld on the owner's decision.
- **Resolution.** Publish a wrapper or rewrite the helper to the documented protocol; test on other systems when machines exist.
- **Owner.** stomp-engine
- **Next step.** None needed before [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)").
- **Evidence.** Pedal control notes

### Gate 2: recovers known presets (end of week 3) (next milestone)

**State: At Risk** · Core method milestone. Show the tool can recover a known answer: given audio made from a preset we already know, its comparison measure picks the true preset and its search finds the right blocks and settings, at thresholds agreed in advance.

- [x] On the known-answer set, the tone-comparison measure ranks the true preset first at the agreed threshold.
- [x] Block types, amp and effect models, and parameter values are identified within the agreed accuracy.
- [x] A curve showing how accuracy falls as guitar separation gets worse has been recorded.

**Demonstrated.** Attempt 3 ran on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items (ledger [2026-10-18T15:46:15](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L1178 "benchmark · native · synthetic-v3 · block_f1 = 0.885417 (first of 8 rows at this time)") (8 rows)): all 7 checks met. Category 0.885 (bar 0.85), model 0.771 (bar 0.60), settings error 0.101 (bar 0.15), ranking the true preset first 0.979, 94 of 96 (bar 0.95; misses i039 and i095, both cabinets), model against the random walk +0.208 (interval low end +0.094, needs -0.05), settings against the random walk -0.083 (whole interval below zero). The consistency split carries no information on this set. The ranking check used the owner's extended rule B (volume-only matches count as ties), amended before the run; attempts 1 and 2 keep their old-rule figures (attempt 1 met 5 of 7, attempt 2 met 6 of 7, rank-1 0.9375). The separation stress curve is ticked from 2026-10-16. Only the owner's sign-off is missing.  
**Remaining.** The owner's sign-off. Later work (not a gate condition): delay model accuracy, drive, and real songs.  
**Blocking.** Awaiting the owner's sign-off (2026-10-18). No check is failing.  
**Next action.** Owner: sign [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") or say what is missing. The gate ledger row is written only with the sign-off.  
Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off; [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95); [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); Baseline "middle of every knob", 96 items (provisional weights, before admission); Hybrid search development runs on the 16 development items (not gate evidence); Tone-comparison admission: second Helix trust set, test split scored once (96 items, 19 look-alikes each); Tone-comparison tuning on the train split only (five stages); Native setting check, baked values: zero violations; Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, setting truth: 116 of 120 pairs, rule 2b 63 of 73; Tone-comparison trust check on real Helix renders (40 known chains, 2,400 renders); Separation stress curve on a synthetic mix (five densities); Gate checklists; Decision log.

### Gate 1: both engines driven by code (end of week 1)

**State: Complete** · Foundational milestone. Prove both sound engines can be driven by code, and that a generated preset loads on the pedal.

- [x] Helix Native renders a test clip from code (smoke test passes).
- [x] The HX Stomp renders the same test clip from code on at least one operating system.
- [x] A generated preset file is accepted by HX Edit and loads on the pedal.
- [x] The shared engine interface is frozen and the control routes are documented.

**Demonstrated.** All four criteria are evidenced and the owner signed off on 2026-10-14. Caveats the owner accepted: the pedal result rested on one operating system and firmware 3.50 (the pedal has since been updated to 3.80 and its smoke test passes there); the pedal-load result was reported by the owner, not observed by an automated test.  
**Remaining.** Nothing for the gate itself. Windows and Linux pedal control remain unverified.  
**Next action.** None. The pedal is now on firmware 3.80 and its smoke test passes there; the remaining 3.80 re-checks (parity with Native) belong to the next gate.  
Evidence: [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") sign-off record; Native smoke test; Pedal smoke test, independent re-run; Generated preset loaded on the pedal (owner-reported).

### Gate 3: blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)

**State: Not Started** · Product-level milestone. Show that the tool can name the right block and settings from the rendered output alone, without ever seeing the dry guitar track that made it.

- [ ] A sealed set of single-block items exists: random model, random settings, rendered in Native from a random held-out guitar sample the matcher never sees.
- [ ] The pass bars and the two baselines (middle of every knob, random walk) are registered before the run, under the same no-dry-track condition.
- [ ] Blind prediction meets the bars and beats both baselines on the sealed set.
- [ ] A short blind listen of the prediction re-rendered from the held-out dry sample agrees.

**Demonstrated.** Nothing yet: no sealed blind set, bars or blind prediction exist. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") passed only with the dry track known, and earlier evidence says the no-dry-track case is the hard part.  
**Remaining.** Finish the no-DI research ([T158](../../board/TASKS.md#t158 "No-DI method survey (human 2026-10-18")), choose the method, build the held-out dry-sample split and the sealed set ([T159](../../board/TASKS.md#t159 "Gate 3 blind single-block set")), register the bars, then run and listen.  
**Blocking.** Not started: blocked on the no-DI research ([T158](../../board/TASKS.md#t158 "No-DI method survey (human 2026-10-18")). Also depends on [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") sign-off by the human.  
**Next action.** Complete research [T158](../../board/TASKS.md#t158 "No-DI method survey (human 2026-10-18"), then build the held-out set ([T159](../../board/TASKS.md#t159 "Gate 3 blind single-block set")).  
Evidence: Gate checklists; Decision log.

### Gate 4: matches clean recordings of real pedals (new, human 2026-10-17; was Gate 3, renumbered 2026-10-18)

**State: Not Started** · Product-level milestone. Show that the tool can match clean recordings of real guitar pedals, judged by re-rendering the matched preset from a held-out dry take and by a short blind listen.

- [ ] A dry reference of the player's guitar (random playing) has been captured; it is the only dry signal the matcher may use.
- [ ] A set of single real pedals (one per available category, several random settings each) has been recorded, with the matching dry take kept aside for scoring.
- [ ] The matched presets meet a quality bar set before the run, and a short blind listen agrees.
- [ ] Two- and three-pedal chains have been recorded and matched by the chain builder at the same bar.

**Demonstrated.** Nothing yet: no dry reference, pedal recording or real-pedal match exists. Some groundwork carries over (the chain builder tested with fake engines, the blind listening tool), but none of it has been run on real pedals.  
**Remaining.** Capture the dry reference and the single-pedal set, choose the quality bar before the run, then record and match two- and three-pedal chains. The matcher must work without a lined-up dry take (note-invariant comparison terms, a learned guitar embedding, a phase-insensitive modulation score).  
**Blocking.** Depends on [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") and on the player recording the pedals.  
**Next action.** Wait for [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)"); the player can plan the recording session.  
Evidence: Gate checklists; Chain builder design and fake-engine tests; Blind A/B listening tool.

### Gate 5: beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)

**State: In Progress** · Product-level milestone. Show that, for real songs, the tool's preset beats the nearest ready-made community preset in blind listening.

- [ ] In blind listening on a 20-song set, the matched preset is preferred over the nearest community preset on most songs.
- [x] The difference between the pedal and Native on firmware 3.80 has been recorded.
- [ ] Native-mode and pedal-mode matching have been compared on the same songs.

**Demonstrated.** One song section was matched and listened to informally (not blind). Pedal-versus-Native agreement on firmware 3.80 is recorded (ticked). Groundwork: a chain-pattern prior from 2,068 curated presets with strong order rules, a block-presence detector (only the cabinet and gain-stage calls pass, so it is a soft hint), a chain builder tested with fake engines, a cabinet-response study, and a blind A/B listening tool (built; no session rated). The Native-versus-pedal comparison on songs has not been run.  
**Remaining.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), the chain builder on a real engine, the pedal verification fixes, the 20-song set, blind sessions, and the Native-versus-pedal comparison on songs. A full-library test is not planned (human decision).  
**Blocking.** Depends on [Gate 4](../../board/GATES.md#gate-4--matches-clean-recordings-of-real-pedals-new-human-2026-10-17-was-gate-3-renumbered-2026-10-18 "Gate 4 — matches clean recordings of real pedals (new, human 2026-10-17; was Gate 3, renumbered 2026-10-18)"), on the chain builder, on the pedal fixes, and on song selection.  
**Next action.** Wait for [Gate 4](../../board/GATES.md#gate-4--matches-clean-recordings-of-real-pedals-new-human-2026-10-17-was-gate-3-renumbered-2026-10-18 "Gate 4 — matches clean recordings of real pedals (new, human 2026-10-17; was Gate 3, renumbered 2026-10-18)"). The owner can start choosing songs now.  
Evidence: Informal listening to the first real-song matches; Pedal versus Native agreement on firmware 3.80 (spot checks); Time-effect agreement measured with test tones (reverbs, modulation, delays); Gray Flanger and Deluxe Phaser agreement on 20 s tones; Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only); Song-context research note (MusicBrainz, opt-in); Preset-corpus chain statistics (category level only); Block-presence detector scored once on its test split (single Helix Native blocks); Chain builder design and fake-engine tests; Blind A/B listening tool; Cabinet impulse-response library analysis (aggregate note, vendor-free); Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing).

## What works and what does not

### Working and verified

| Capability | Current reality |
|---|---|
| Control Helix Native from code | Working on macOS with Native 3.82; a 2-second clip renders in about 0.28 s with memory steady. |
| Control the HX Stomp from code and restore it | Working on macOS, firmware 3.80; a whole preset is written, read back and checked, the pedal is restored exactly, and its blocks stay editable afterwards. Not yet checked: parity with Native on 3.80. |
| Read, write and check preset files | Byte-for-byte round trip on both reference files; a smoke-test preset loaded on the pedal (reported by the owner). |
| Catch a mismatch between what was written and what the pedal holds | Every parameter is compared after each write; this found a delay bug that is now fixed. |
| Native and the pedal agree on simple presets | Three of four tested presets agree closely; the fourth, with an added effect, differs more. Breaks for one cabinet setting. |
| Hold the pedal's effect models in Native as single-block templates | 605 of the pedal's 607 models captured (the other 2 are refused by Native 3.82) and every template replayed in a fresh host; delay times move in 10 ms steps. Measured on one Mac; nothing judged by ear. |
| Native stands in for the pedal on most effect types | Measured on amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, most drives, delays, four reverb types, Hall above 500 Hz, tremolo, chorus, vibrato, flanger and phaser. The pedal still checks Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and Early Reflections. |
| Render two variants in one pedal pass | A two-variant pass equals serial renders on firmware 3.80: path A -78.5 and -85.1 dB, path B -60.4 and -78.6 dB, each below the serial repeat floor, at about 1.7 s per variant (earlier: about 2.9 s). Measured on one pedal and two chain pairs. |
| Restore the pedal after a session, judged by decoded equality | The restored preset decodes identical to the backup. Raw bytes differed by 3 because the pedal re-encodes after another preset is selected, so the byte check is secondary. The offline re-check used hardware buffers. |
| Native applies the requested settings exactly and repeats renders | Repeatability 216 of 216 (worst -161.5 dB); zero unexplained start-up differences in 65 hosts; the in-run sentinel catches a forced drift with 0 unforced drifts in 9,770 renders. Setting truth 116 of 120 pairs; the misses are explained (one-step rounding, input/output blocks). One Mac, Native 3.82. |

### Implemented but not yet proven

| Capability | Current reality |
|---|---|
| Pick a preset that matches a recorded tone | One song section tried; the score could not separate the top two choices; the listener's preference was informal. |
| Judge tone similarity reliably | One measure is admitted for single blocks when the clean recording is known: 0.990 on a held-out Helix split against a 0.90 bar (older measure 0.781). With separation stand-in audio 0.948, not admitted. Without a known recording the earlier tests failed (about 7 of 40). Not tested on real songs; the ear has not judged it. |
| Isolate the guitar from a full mix | Runs in seconds; quality scored only on an artificial mix (21.2 dB guitar alone, 7.3 dB with a second guitar); not scored on a real song. |
| Choose a clean reference guitar recording | Picks from a library of 104 recordings; the benefit is unmeasured and the score varies a lot with the clip. |
| Search for blocks and settings | Hybrid search on all 96 known-answer items: right model 0.646, right category 0.802, settings error 0.132 on right-model items. Passes four of six [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") criteria and fails two; strong on drive, cabinet, compressor and delay, weak on modulation, reverb and amp. Synthetic known-answer data on Native; not real songs; the ear has not judged it. |
| Render a chain one block at a time | One 8-block mono test chain agreed to within 67 dB above 20 Hz; dual cabinets and stereo untested. |
| Measure each block's behaviour with a test signal | Fingerprints exist for 602 of 605 Native library models (24 settings silent by design, such as effect loops without a return); whether they improve model choice is not yet shown. |
| Record the player's own guitar alongside the song | Built and tested with fake devices; some takes exist; accuracy not measured. |
| Predict typical chain order | A bigram prior from 2,068 curated presets with strong order rules (drive before amp, amp before cab, delay before reverb) and super-groups. Aggregate statistics only; not yet used by a chain builder on a real engine. |
| Detect which kinds of block a tone contains | Scored once on a test split of single Helix Native blocks: only the cabinet and the presence of a gain stage pass; delay, reverb and modulation miss. Used as a soft hint. |
| Estimate a cabinet by deconvolution | Promising on a real clip when the amp is right (the pedal cabinet is the top match in 22 of 24); useless with the wrong amp. Not yet part of a search. |
| Build a whole chain from analysis | A chain builder exists and passes its tests with fake engines only; it has not been run on a real engine. |
| Pedal renders are sample-stable and settings land exactly | Not shown. Setting truth 20 of 40 and repeatability 12 of 17 on the pedal under the fixed rules; a one-sample slip occurs in about 1 render in 90; four models are refused. The longer pre-roll is accepted, but no pedal result counts until the slip is handled. |

### Missing or not working

| Capability | Current reality |
|---|---|
| Turn a song into a pedal-verified preset with one command | Not implemented; the steps are run by hand. |
| Produce up to three tone snapshots from one song | Not implemented end to end. |
| Learned predictor for good starting points | Not started. |
| Pedal control on Windows or Linux | Unverified; no machine available. |
| Stereo blocks and dual cabinets in Native | The Native host is mono only. |
| Run blind listening sessions | The session tool is built; no session has been prepared or rated. |
| Look up song context | An opt-in lookup is designed and a 3-song live probe was reported; years and tags need a second lookup. Nothing is wired into matching. |
| Pass the known-answer test ([Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)")) | Attempt 1 ran on all 96 items and did not pass (category 0.802 against 0.85; model choice not shown no worse than the random walk). Attempt 2 needs a fresh set and per-family specialists. |
| A specialist for each effect family | Decided on 2026-10-17; none built yet. Order: modulation, reverb and delay, then amp. |

## Human decisions and actions

One list, each action once. Routine work owned by the agents is not listed here.

### Blocking now

- **Sign [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), or decline to** (High)  
  Why: Attempt 3 met all seven checks on the sealed set. Only the owner can sign; the gate ledger row is written only with that sign-off.  
  Unblocks: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") complete, and readiness can move toward Validated.  
  Recommended: Read the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") page (verdict, rule-B amendment, delay misses), listen to a few target-left, guess-right files, then sign if you accept the caveats: known-clean-recording case only, ranking rule amended before the run, three sealed sets consumed.  
  If deferred: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") stays open and later gates keep waiting on it.

### Needed soon

- **Choose the first real songs for local testing** (High)  
  Why: Separation quality on real songs is unmeasured and the final test needs 20 songs. Pick a few now spanning clean to high-gain tones; the full 20 can follow.  
  Unblocks: The separation stress test and the song set for [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)").  
  Recommended: Three to five songs now, local only; audio never leaves the machine.  
  If deferred: Separation risk stays unmeasured and [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") cannot be scheduled.
- **Decide whether to commit and push the accumulated work** (Medium)  
  Why: A large body of specialist, job-runner, report and [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") evidence work exists locally and the public repository does not have it.  
  Unblocks: A saved, reviewable history; the public status page stays current.  
  Recommended: Commit and push after the lead confirms the full suite is green.  
  If deferred: Work stays local and unreviewed; a lost disk loses it.
- **Record the play-along takes and report what happened** (Medium)  
  Why: The tool can record your own clean guitar alongside the song. The first takes exist, but the board does not say which commands ran on the pedal or whether the count-in and alignment were right. The clean guitar used strongly changes the score (7.7 to 22.7, lower is better), so this is the cleanest way to remove that variable.  
  Unblocks: Measuring the effect of the clean-guitar choice and the accuracy mode that uses your own recording.  
  Recommended: Record the DS-Sample section again with a guitar in the pedal's Main L/Mono input and note whether the click and alignment were right.  
  If deferred: The clean-guitar effect stays unmeasured; the search keeps using a library recording.
- **Provide about 130 songs with known artist and title for the song-context test** (Medium)  
  Why: Song lookups (MusicBrainz, opt-in, artist and title only) were approved. A live probe found 3 of 3 songs, but years and tags need a second lookup, so the larger test needs a list with reliable names.  
  Unblocks: Measuring whether artist and title give a useful prior for the chain builder and search.  
  Recommended: A plain list of artist and title pairs; no audio is needed.  
  If deferred: The song-context idea stays unmeasured; nothing else depends on it.

### Later / optional

- **Check the delay settings on the pedal after loading a regenerated preset** (Low)  
  Why: A bug that made the delay too wet was fixed, and the pedal now reads delay Time and Mix back exactly after a write. A look at the pedal screen would add a human confirmation.  
  Unblocks: Trust in the pedal read-back step that the final check depends on.  
  Recommended: Load the regenerated high-gain test preset and read the delay Time, Feedback and Mix on the pedal screen.  
  If deferred: No consequence; the read-back already covers it.
- **Provide a Windows and a Linux machine, or accept macOS only** (Low)  
  Why: Pedal control is verified on macOS only.  
  Unblocks: Cross-platform claims.  
  Recommended: Decide when the pipeline works; macOS-only is acceptable until then.  
  If deferred: The tool is documented as macOS-only for pedal mode.
- **Record what the Line 6 licence agreements say about training and automation** (Low)  
  Why: The reading was done but the findings are not written down. The owner decided on 2026-10-17 that trained weights may be published; the recorded licence findings are the backing for that.  
  Unblocks: The learning stage, which is not near.  
  Recommended: Add the findings to the licence notes in a few lines.  
  If deferred: Learning cannot start safely, but nothing earlier depends on it.
- **Confirm the copyright line in the project licence** (Low)  
  Why: The project is MIT licensed but the copyright holder line is unconfirmed.  
  Unblocks: A clean public release.  
  Recommended: Confirm or edit the name.  
  If deferred: None before release.
- **Open newly generated presets in HX Edit and report errors** (Low)  
  Why: Only HX Edit can confirm the file is accepted; an error code -8309 means the wrong device.  
  Unblocks: Confidence that generated files import cleanly.  
  Recommended: Do it for the final candidate presets only, not every experiment.  
  If deferred: Import problems would be found later.
- **Read the Discogs terms if Discogs is wanted as a second source** (Low)  
  Why: The approved source is MusicBrainz only. Discogs would add artist-gear detail, but its terms have not been read for this use.  
  Unblocks: A possible second metadata source.  
  Recommended: Only read them if MusicBrainz proves too thin.  
  If deferred: None; MusicBrainz is enough to start.

Delay (5 of 12 exact models) is now the weakest effect. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off; Decision log; Gate checklists)_
- 2026-10-18 · **New problem.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 did not pass: 6 of 7 checks on the 96 sealed items. Category 0.865 (was 0.802), model 0.760 (was 0.646), settings error 0.091, better than the random walk on both model and settings, consistency split passes. Ranking the true preset first fails at 0.9375 against 0.95 (was 0.990): six misses on amp, drive and one cabinet, which the middle baseline shares. Modulation and drive are weakest; cabinet and equaliser are perfect. The rule is unchanged and v2 is now seen. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human approved the next improvement round for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3: a gain-stage score term, drive and modulation specialists, and a fresh sealed synthetic-v3 (generated and sealed since). Synthetic-v2 is now spent: diagnosis only, never used to admit a change. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Specialists were adopted for four families: cabinet and equaliser (model 24 of 24 on development items against 18 of 24 for the general method), time-based effects (held-out model 14 to 19), amp (param error 0.224 to 0.120), and a dispatcher plus adapters that route each family to its specialist. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 ran on all of them; category and model rose accordingly. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Parallel jobs now survive restarts (an item-level queue, workers grow and shrink with the host scaler), the Native host cap was raised from 6 to 8 on the human's instruction, and a false silence alarm on a very quiet true preset (item i062) was fixed; i062 stays in the set as a normal item. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 did not pass. On all 96 items: model 0.646, settings error 0.132, ranking 0.990 and settings better than the random walk pass; category 0.802 (bar 0.85) and model non-inferiority to the random walk (low end -0.083, needs -0.05) fail. The 80 untuned items agree. Strong families: drive, cabinet, compressor, delay; weak: modulation, reverb, amp. The next attempt uses a fresh set and per-family specialists. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human directed a specialist per effect family, with single blocks first and chains built from analysis, no full-library test, trained weights may be published, and the cabinet-response library may be used for training. The 19 non-impulse-response recordings were removed from the library folder to the Trash. The pass rule was frozen before the run, and the baseline "random search" is now called the random walk. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Native is verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"): three engine bugs were fixed (baked controls, hosts in the other rounding mode, an amp state latch), repeatability is 216 of 216, nothing is unexplained, and an in-run sentinel now guards long jobs. Recorded misses (116 of 120 setting-truth pairs, 63 of 73 baked renders) are one-step rounding and input/output blocks. _(Evidence: Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, setting truth: 116 of 120 pairs, rule 2b 63 of 73; Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Native in-run sentinel catches a forced numeric-mode drift; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** The pedal verification found real sampling defects: unstable USB audio for about 0.7 s after start, amp state carried between renders, four models refused, and a slow in-place glide on some delay and reverb settings. A longer pre-roll fixed the first; a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. No pedal result counts until it is handled. _(Evidence: Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing); Pedal verification, last full run on the longer pre-roll: setting truth 20 of 40; sentinel tripped on a real one-sample slip; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Search got measured seeding (the target's own delay time, reverb decay and modulation rate set the starting point) and then a hybrid method with broad draws, narrowing and refinement, chosen by a rule fixed before the last run. On the 16 development items model accuracy went from 6 to 13 of 16 over the day (development only, not gate evidence). _(Evidence: Hybrid search development runs on the 16 development items (not gate evidence); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Baselines were recorded: the middle-of-every-knob guess and the random walk. The random walk scores high because it has 300 tries over a 47-model pool judged by the trusted score. _(Evidence: Baseline "middle of every knob", 96 items (provisional weights, before admission); [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** The curated preset corpus (2,068 presets after cleaning) gave strong order rules, super-groups and heavy concentration of model use; descriptive metadata adds little. Only category-level statistics are committed. _(Evidence: Preset-corpus chain statistics (category level only); Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** The cabinet impulse-response library analysis shows that estimating a cabinet by deconvolution is promising: the pedal's cabinet is the top match in 22 of 24 real-clip tests when the amp is right. It is useless with the wrong amp. No library data leaves the machine. _(Evidence: Cabinet impulse-response library analysis (aggregate note, vendor-free); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** The block-presence detector, scored once on its test split, passed only for the cabinet and for whether a gain stage is present. It is used as a soft hint only; time-based blocks are confirmed by rendering with and without them. _(Evidence: Block-presence detector scored once on its test split (single Helix Native blocks); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Resolved.** The two engine items that were held back (out-of-range values and a real-guitar smoke test) are done: the real-guitar smoke test passes on Native. On-demand capture defaults off after it opened an editor window per preset. _(Evidence: Native smoke test on a real guitar clip; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Resolved.** The Native verification suite first showed one of 216 repeat renders at -97.4 dB against the -100 dB line. The cause was a state carried between models (an amp latch), plus baked controls and a rounding mode. After three engine fixes repeatability is 216 of 216 (worst -161.5 dB), with an in-run sentinel in place. _(Evidence: Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, baked values: zero violations; Native in-run sentinel catches a forced numeric-mode drift)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** A chain-pattern prior was built from 129 presets: drive comes before amp in 55 of 55 presets with both, amp before cab 90 of 90, delay before reverb in 96 percent. It is aggregate statistics only; no preset content is committed. It is not yet used by a chain builder. _(Evidence: Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only))_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** New direction from the human: single blocks first, with chains built from analysis of real presets (which kinds of block are present, ordered by common patterns). The curated preset and cabinet-IR libraries stay local and are never committed; the song-metadata lookup (artist and title only) is approved as opt-in. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** The tone comparison was admitted for single blocks when the clean recording is known. Tuned on a train split and scored once on a held-out split of 96 items, it ranked the true preset first 0.990 of the time against a bar of 0.90 (older measure 0.781), counting identical-sounding look-alikes as ties. The tuning score matched the test score, so it was not overfit. This replaces the failed first check as the main evidence for that case only. _(Evidence: Tone-comparison admission: second Helix trust set, test split scored once (96 items, 19 look-alikes each); Tone-comparison tuning on the train split only (five stages))_
- 2026-10-16 · **New problem.** Model choice tied the old pool pass at 7 of 16 development models under the old provisional score. This was superseded on 2026-10-17: the baselines are now recorded and the hybrid search replaced it. _(Evidence: Baseline "middle of every knob", 96 items (provisional weights, before admission); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Native block fingerprints were measured for 602 of 605 library models (24 of about 3,600 settings were silent, all expected), and rendering many candidates at once is 1.46 times faster than one at a time with identical results. Model choice per slot is built. _(Evidence: Native block fingerprints: 602 models, 24 expected-silent settings)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The pedal's two-variant pass equals serial renders on firmware 3.80 and is about 1.7 times faster per variant (1.70 s against about 2.9 s). This closes the audio check that was open last report. Pedal restores are now judged by decoded equality, since raw bytes can differ by 3 after the pedal re-encodes. _(Evidence: Pedal two-variant pass equals serial renders, about 1.7 s per variant (firmware 3.80); Pedal restore judged by decoded equality (offline re-check of the hardware buffers))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human chose the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") scoring rules (tune the measure for the known-recording case; count identical-sounding look-alikes as ties) and waived the 'right chain structure' target for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), which now covers single blocks only. Chain structure moves to [Gate 4](../../board/GATES.md#gate-4--matches-clean-recordings-of-real-pedals-new-human-2026-10-17-was-gate-3-renumbered-2026-10-18 "Gate 4 — matches clean recordings of real pedals (new, human 2026-10-17; was Gate 3, renumbered 2026-10-18)"). Scoring rules 2 and 3 are coded. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **New problem.** The first trust check on real Helix sound failed: no tone-comparison measure picks the true preset reliably. On new notes the best managed about 7 times in 40 against a bar of 36; the measures react more to the notes played than to the tone. With the same notes and a lined-up recording one measure reached 0.725 (0.875 if look-alikes that sound identical are ignored). This replaces the earlier stand-in result as the main evidence and is now the biggest problem. _(Evidence: Tone-comparison trust check on real Helix renders (40 known chains, 2,400 renders))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The software engine now holds 605 of the pedal's 607 effect models as single-block templates (the other 2 are refused by Native). Every template was replayed in a fresh host. This opens model choice from a library, but the blocks are not yet measured. _(Evidence: Native block library: 605 of 605 pedal models captured)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Delay times in the software engine now move in 10 ms steps instead of 63 ms, because Native honours a narrower control range. Block Level was also given a finer range (0.25 dB steps with 0 dB on the grid). _(Evidence: Narrower control ranges honoured by Native (delay Time in 10 ms steps); Block Level range \[-25.75, +6\] dB and re-captured templates)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The measurements settled where the software engine can stand in for the pedal: amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, most drives, delays, four reverb types, Hall above 500 Hz and the modulation effects. The pedal still checks Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and Early Reflections. A suggested delay-time correction of under 1 in 100 was rejected: the apparent error was Native's control step, not an engine difference. _(Evidence: Time-effect agreement measured with test tones (reverbs, modulation, delays); Gray Flanger and Deluxe Phaser agreement on 20 s tones; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Two chains in one pedal pass give the same sound as one chain per pass on firmware 3.80, and code to render two chains per pass was written. Its audio comparison has not run because the Mac's audio failed during the session. Update: the audio comparison was later run and passed (see the 2026-10-16 entry on the pedal's two-variant pass). _(Evidence: Two-chain pass versus single-chain pass on the pedal, firmware 3.80; Pedal engine push: control-only session (two-chain writes, layout change, readback); Pedal session: every audio stream failed (CoreAudio down))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The separation stress curve is recorded on a synthetic mix: guitar-only quality 21.2 dB falls to 7.3 dB with a second guitar. This ticks one [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") item but says nothing yet about real songs. _(Evidence: Separation stress curve on a synthetic mix (five densities))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The search keeps scoring the full clip: shorter clips did worse in the clip-length study. Two-chain passes on Native are out of scope (decided by the owner), and time-variant blocks are opt-in for search. _(Evidence: Clip-length study for the search; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Checking candidates on the pedal got much faster: a search trial went from 10.9 s to 3.8 s and the per-test session overhead from 3.81 s to 0.77 s. A verification finalist improved less, from 29.2 s to 24.7 s. The search now uses these options by default (task [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…")), but how much that speeds a real search has not yet been timed on the pedal. These timings are on one pedal with one chain type; they say nothing about match quality. _(Evidence: Pedal search trial time, before and after; Pedal session overhead, before and after; Pedal verification time per finalist, before and after; Pedal speed measurements and the flash-slot proposal)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Helix Native's speed work ([T055](../../board/TASKS.md#t055 "Native phase 3: parallel render pool for search")) is finished. Capturing a new chain fell from about 31 s to 1.2 s in one session and 0.39 s with three sessions (216 blocks in 84.6 s). A pool of 6 parallel hosts runs 9.9 two-second tests per second (4.6 on ten-second clips); 10 hosts did a little better but with memory pressure and swapping, so the cap is 6. Bypass by MIDI control still matches separate captures (-328.7 dB), and a fault where about 1 host in 10 rendered differently is now caught by a start-up check. Measured on one Mac; nothing here has been judged by ear. _(Evidence: Native capture speed-up (216 chains); Native render pool, 2 to 10 hosts (6 is the cap); Native bypass over MIDI control re-checked; Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Native engine notes, phase 3)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Follow-up Native work ([T064](../../board/TASKS.md#t064 "Native phase 3 follow-ups")): the two template sets used for matching (B and B0) were re-captured with Hum and Ripple at 0, so the matcher has all 7 test candidates under both engine policies. A scan of 607 pedal block models found only 2 that Native 3.82 refuses to load, so nearly the whole catalog can be captured for Native; the library itself is still not captured. _(Evidence: Native templates B and B0 re-captured at the frozen values; Refused-model scan: 2 of 607 pedal models refused by Native 3.82)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Resolved.** The pedal's two-path layout difference is fixed ([T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80")). Our two-path version of a stored preset used to cancel against the one saved by the pedal's editor at only -9.3 dB; it now cancels at -85.7 dB, the same as the editor's preset repeated against itself. Single-path loads are unchanged. Measured on firmware 3.80, one preset, one pedal. _(Evidence: Two-path layout fix: -9.3 dB before, -85.7 dB after; Two-path pedal render differs from single-path (and snapshot recall check))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The rule that two-path renders may only be compared with two-path renders is lifted, because after the layout fix a two-path pass matches single-chain renders (Path A -72.9 dB against a repeat floor of -68.7; Path B -52.6 against -42.7). Two chains per pass can be used for checks; the 2.0x gain was measured on firmware 3.50 and is not re-measured on 3.80. _(Evidence: Two-path layout fix: -9.3 dB before, -85.7 dB after; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: the amp's Hum and Ripple settings are frozen at 0 on both engines. They added a free-running noise that made two renders of the same preset differ at about -30 dB (against -72 to -91 dB at 0), which hid real differences and blocked the shorter pedal waits. This closes the open question and removes it from the owner's action list. _(Evidence: Pedal renders with amp Hum and Ripple on versus at 0; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: Helix Native runs at most 6 hosts at once (measured on 32 GB of memory: green memory pressure at 6, yellow with swapping at 8 and 10). All Native pool figures in this report are for 6 hosts. _(Evidence: Native render pool, 2 to 10 hosts (6 is the cap); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history and not re-run wholesale. About 1 host in 10 had rendered differently and delay trims could differ by up to 30 samples. The results we rely on are re-measured in the parity spot checks and the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") benchmark. _(Evidence: Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Resolved.** The pedal's blocks no longer stay locked after a control session. The helper now ends each session the way the pedal's own editing program does; a person confirmed the blocks were editable afterwards on firmware 3.80, where the lock had still occurred before the fix. _(Evidence: Pedal blocks still locked on firmware 3.80 before the fix; Pedal blocks editable after a session (owner-observed, firmware 3.80); Pedal smoke test on firmware 3.80, session close answered)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** The pedal now runs firmware 3.80. Its engine smoke test passes there, and 9 of 9 whole-preset writes (including amps with impulse-response cabinets and one block from each effect category) landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte. Pedal-versus-Native agreement has not yet been re-measured on 3.80. _(Evidence: Pedal smoke test on firmware 3.80, session close answered; Whole-preset writes on firmware 3.80, 9 of 9, pedal restored)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** Helix Native now loads new sounds without a person at the keyboard and renders about seven times faster, and it stopped running out of memory. _(Evidence: Native memory and speed fix)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** The pedal write is now checked parameter by parameter. This traced and fixed a bug that made a delay run far too wet when the owner played a preset live on the pedal. _(Evidence: Pedal write read-back check; Live check of the delay fix)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** A first real song section was matched end to end and loaded on the pedal. The result was informal progress, not a validation. _(Evidence: Informal listening to the first real-song matches)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** Rendering a chain one block at a time matched rendering it whole on a test chain, which opens the way to measuring blocks individually. _(Evidence: Block-by-block rendering test)_
- 2026-10-15 · **New problem.** A new problem was found: one cabinet setting sounds different on the pedal and in Native. Presets for the pedal keep it at 0 for now. _(Evidence: Pedal versus Native agreement on four presets; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **New problem.** The first real-song run showed the tone score is not reliable: its top two candidates were almost tied and the listener preferred one clearly. _(Evidence: Informal listening to the first real-song matches)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Resolved.** A silent bug that could reuse an outdated Native setup (and made one measurement look much worse than it was) was found and fixed; the false result was voided. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The owner approved the per-block approach (measure and search block by block). It adds work before [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") but shrinks the search space. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-14 · **Milestone.** [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") was signed off: both engines are driven by code and a generated preset loads on the pedal. _(Evidence: [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") sign-off record)_

### In progress now

- **Delay accuracy (next target).** Delay is now the weakest effect (5 of 12 exact models, mostly one delay mistaken for another). Next step is diagnosis on development sets; the sealed sets v1, v2 and v3 are all spent. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off; Decision log)_
- **Pedal sampling fixes.** Handle the residual one-sample slip (about 1 in 90 renders), the four refused models and the in-place glide. Pre-roll is already fixed. No pedal result counts until then. _(Evidence: Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing); Pedal verification, last full run on the longer pre-roll: setting truth 20 of 40; sentinel tripped on a real one-sample slip; Decision log)_
- **Chain building from analysis.** The chain builder exists with fake engines; the next step is a multi-block render set on Native and judging post-amp blocks against the current best render. _(Evidence: Chain builder design and fake-engine tests; Block-presence detector scored once on its test split (single Helix Native blocks); Decision log)_
- **Song context.** Opt-in MusicBrainz lookups (artist and title only). A 3-song live probe found all 3, but years and tags need a second lookup. _(Evidence: Song-context research note (MusicBrainz, opt-in); Decision log)_

### Up next

- **[Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") sign-off.** The owner reviews the attempt 3 result and signs [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"). _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off; Gate checklists)_
- **Listen to the delay misses.** Target-left, guess-right files for the delay misses and some wins are on the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") page. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); awaiting the human's sign-off)_
- **Pedal re-verification.** Register the revised pedal rules before the re-run, then re-measure earlier pedal agreement figures that used the short pre-roll. _(Evidence: Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing); Decision log)_
- **Chain builder on a real engine.** Use cabinet estimation by deconvolution as the second stage and the presence hint as a soft prior; confirm time-based blocks by rendering with and without them. _(Evidence: Chain builder design and fake-engine tests; Cabinet impulse-response library analysis (aggregate note, vendor-free); Decision log)_

### Activity that did not advance readiness

- 2026-10-17 · A chain builder (tested with fake engines) and a blind A/B listening tool were built, and a local [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") explainer for a non-technical reader was added (kept off the public repository). None of these changes a gate on its own.
- 2026-10-16 · The known-answer harness (96 one-block presets) was built and a 3-item run made. The run is provisional and not gate evidence; its first version scored the true preset against itself and was voided and re-run.
- 2026-10-16 · Pedal engine work: a shared pedal lock with a device check, layout-changing loads written whole with read-back, and the Simple Delay Scale report explained (not a fault). A pedal session had control steps pass but audio fail (Mac audio down); the pedal was restored. These are not yet committed.
- 2026-10-16 · The continuous-integration build (the automatic test run on GitHub) was fixed in commit 07b66ed: one test that needs a local-only file is skipped when the file is absent. It changes no product capability.
- 2026-10-15 · The block measuring kit was finished offline. It has not touched Helix yet, so it does not advance readiness until it is run.
- 2026-10-14 · Two extra tone-comparison measures were added (a sharper loudness-shape measure and a learned audio embedding). Neither came close to the target on stand-in signals, so they did not move [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") forward.
