| [Foundations: installation, licensing and the working process](#foundations-installation-licensing-and-the-working-process) | **Working** | unit-tests |

### Sound engines: Helix Native and the HX Stomp pedal

**Working.** Turn a candidate preset and a clean guitar clip into sound, so candidates can be compared. Native is fast and offline; the pedal is the real thing.

- **What works.** Both engines are driven from code on macOS. Native is verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"): exact settings apart from explained misses, 216 of 216 repeat renders, a sentinel against drift. The pedal two-variant pass, whole-preset writes and decoded-equality restores work.
- **What is missing.** Windows and Linux, stereo blocks, the cabinet Early Reflections setting, some reverb and drive types. Pedal verification is incomplete: four refused models, a slow glide on some settings, and a one-sample slip in about 1 render in 90.
- **Primary problem.** No pedal measurement counts until the residual slip is handled.
- **Next step.** Detect or re-take slipped pedal renders, then fix the refusals and the glide, then re-register and re-run the pedal rules.
- **Evidence confidence.** Native: hardware-measured on one Mac, 9,770 renders in the verification. Pedal: hardware, firmware 3.80, with recorded failures; ear not involved.
- **Detail.** [Helix Native engine](#helix-native-engine), [HX Stomp engine and hardware reamp](#hx-stomp-engine-and-hardware-reamp), [Engine interface and selection](#engine-interface-and-selection)

### Preset files and the block catalog

**Partially Working.** Read, write and validate the preset files the pedal and Native load.

- **What works.** Reference files round-trip exactly, models are read from the owner's own install, generated files load, and a chain-pattern prior built from 2,068 curated presets (category-level statistics only) shows strong order rules.
- **What is missing.** Building up to three snapshots from a match, and forcing snapshot values to equal block values inside the writer.
- **Primary problem.** A stale snapshot value can silently change the sound; the rule 'block values win' is applied in scripts but not yet in the writer.
- **Next step.** Move the snapshot synchronisation into the preset writer.
- **Evidence confidence.** Round trip tested on two reference files; one generated preset loaded on the pedal.
- **Detail.** [Preset generation and validation (.hlx, catalog import)](#preset-generation-and-validation-hlx-catalog-import)

### Preparing the song: guitar isolation and a clean reference guitar

**Partially Working.** Extract the guitar from the recording and supply a clean guitar recording to compare against.

- **What works.** Separation runs in seconds, sections are found and a library of clean guitar clips is indexed. A stress curve on a synthetic mix is recorded.
- **What is missing.** Any quality score on real songs and any measurement of the clean-guitar choice's benefit.
- **Primary problem.** Separation quality on real songs is unknown.
- **Next step.** Stress-test on a few real songs once the owner chooses them.
- **Evidence confidence.** Synthetic mix only; one real song without a quality score.
- **Detail.** [Guitar separation and section segmentation](#guitar-separation-and-section-segmentation), [Reference DI library (dry signal)](#reference-di-library-dry-signal)

### Judging tone similarity: comparison measures and block measuring

**Partially Working.** Say how close two guitar sounds are, and describe how each block behaves.

- **What works.** Five comparison measures run. One (the multi-resolution spectral measure, version 2) is admitted for single blocks with a known clean recording (0.990). A block-presence detector exists (cabinet and gain stage pass; the rest miss). A cabinet-response estimator is promising. Block fingerprints exist for 602 models.
- **What is missing.** A measure for the no-recording song case; reliable detection of delay, reverb and modulation blocks.
- **Primary problem.** The comparison is admitted only for the known-recording case. The score ranks the true preset first on 90 of 96 sealed items (bar 0.95): gain-stage neighbours score alike.
- **Next step.** Add a gain-stage score term that passes loss-trust on gain neighbours (running).
- **Evidence confidence.** Real Helix renders from Native; held-out splits scored once; synthetic known-answer data, not real songs or ears.
- **Detail.** [Loss kit (tone metrics)](#loss-kit-tone-metrics), [Block probe and fingerprints](#block-probe-and-fingerprints), [Block-presence detector](#block-presence-detector), [Cabinet estimation by deconvolution](#cabinet-estimation-by-deconvolution)

### Searching for the preset and the song-to-preset pipeline

**Partially Working.** Try candidate presets, score them against the target and return the best preset.

- **What works.** A hybrid method with family specialists (cabinet and equaliser, time-based, modulation, amp, gain) runs on Native. On 96 sealed items it named the category 88.5 percent and the model 77.1 percent of the time with settings error 0.101 (attempt 3). A chain builder exists, tested with fake engines only.
- **What is missing.** Delay (model 5 of 12) and drive (7 of 12) are weak. Chain builder on a real engine, snapshots for several sections, a one-command tool.
- **Primary problem.** Weak on delay and drive; delay is the biggest gap now.
- **Next step.** Diagnose and strengthen the delay specialist on development data.
- **Evidence confidence.** Synthetic known-answer data (96 sealed items) on Native; one informal listen on one real song.
- **Detail.** [Search and optimiser](#search-and-optimiser), [End-to-end song-to-preset pipeline and CLI](#end-to-end-song-to-preset-pipeline-and-cli), [Chain builder](#chain-builder), [Per-family specialists](#per-family-specialists)

### Testing, benchmarks and listening

**Partially Working.** Provide the measurements and listening tests that decide whether the product works.

- **What works.** The evidence ledger, smoke tests, engine agreement measurements, the 96-item known-answer set with its runner and frozen pass rule, three full attempt results (the third meets all checks), and a blind A/B session tool.
- **What is missing.** The human's [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") sign-off, fresh sets for later work, a rated blind session.
- **Primary problem.** Three sealed sets are now spent; [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") is awaiting sign-off.
- **Next step.** Prepare listening on the delay misses; build development sets for delay work.
- **Evidence confidence.** Hardware measurements of engine agreement; full known-answer runs on Native; listening informal only.
- **Detail.** [Benchmark, ledger and gates (evaluation)](#benchmark-ledger-and-gates-evaluation), [Blind listening sessions and export](#blind-listening-sessions-and-export)

### Learning from data

**Not Started.** Learn good starting points so the search needs fewer renders.

- **What works.** Nothing.
- **What is missing.** Everything; it depends on a trusted comparison and on the licence findings.
- **Primary problem.** Not applicable yet.
- **Next step.** None until [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)").
- **Evidence confidence.** None.
- **Detail.** [Learning (predictor, proxies)](#learning-predictor-proxies)

### Foundations: installation, licensing and the working process

**Working.** Make the project installable, legally clean and organised.

- **What works.** Installs on this Mac, licence checks pass, and the board, evidence ledger and gates are in use.
- **What is missing.** Windows and Linux checks, recorded licence-agreement findings, and tidier board lines.
- **Primary problem.** Licence-agreement findings are unrecorded; some board lines are stale.
- **Next step.** Update the board; owner records the licence findings.
- **Evidence confidence.** Tests and a licence scan; one operating system.
- **Detail.** [Platform, install and packaging](#platform-install-and-packaging), [Licensing and legal hygiene](#licensing-and-legal-hygiene), [Agent team, board and process](#agent-team-board-and-process)

## Engines: what each can do and how fast

Times are in seconds (s), minutes (min) and hours (h). A render is one pass of a test signal through a preset, recorded as audio. A score is one comparison of a rendered candidate against the target tone (lower is better). A capture is the one-off step that teaches Helix Native a new chain, by loading the preset file into its editor and recording its state. A parity check renders one preset on both engines and compares the two recordings. Estimates are 100 times the newest measured time; every estimate says which measurement it uses and its date. Native pool figures use 6 hosts (the owner approved that cap on 2026-10-16) and count a render plus its score as one test; pedal figures from firmware 3.80 are marked as such.

### What each engine can do

| Capability | HX Stomp (the pedal) | Helix Native (software) |
|---|---|---|
| Load a generated preset file (.hlx) directly | Yes. The file is compiled and written whole into the pedal's working memory, then read back parameter by parameter before anything is rendered; about 40 whole writes, all read back as written ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp T056 outcomes](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-t056-outcomes-lead "2026-10-15 (Stomp T056 outcomes, lead)")). 4 of 4 generated files were written and rendered (ledger [2026-10-14T23:47:43+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L75 "smoke · stomp · smoke · whole_preset_rendered = 4.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). Re-checked on firmware 3.80: 9 of 9 whole writes landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Takes 0.53 s. | Yes, with no person, on macOS: a script drops the file into Native's editor and records the state as a template, then renders from the template (ledger [2026-10-14T23:58:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L88 "smoke · native · smoke · drop_load_null_vs_knob_db = -315.7 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). A stored template loads in 0.15 s; a chain never seen before needs a capture first (see below). |
| Export or save a preset file | Our own writer produces the .hlx; a human imports it in HX Edit (accepted, ledger [2026-10-14T17:38:00+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L21 "smoke · - · known.hlx · hx_edit_import_ok = 1.0")). The engine never saves to the pedal's flash memory, by policy: it edits the working copy only and puts the original preset back ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 stomp-engine, T003 hardware batch and T004](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t003-hardware-batch-and-t004-for-the-lead-to-confirm-at-plan-sync "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T003 hardware batch and T004; for the lead to confirm at plan-sync)")). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Not done and not needed for matching: the generated .hlx is the source, and Native's captured state is kept as a local template, not exported as a file. |
| Change any parameter | Yes, at full resolution. Parameters are set in place and checked by read-back to within one hundred-thousandth of the parameter's range ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine, T051 whole-write readback](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-engine-t051-whole-write-readback "2026-10-15 (stomp-engine, T051 whole-write readback)")). Re-checked on firmware 3.80: 20 in-place loads of six random amp parameters each ended with a clean full read-back (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L258 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · param_load_s_readback_every1 = 0.1955 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). | Only parameters given a MIDI control number at capture, in 128 steps across each range; everything else stays as captured. Inside that grid it equals a freshly captured file almost exactly (ledger [2026-10-15T20:46:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L151 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · delay_1ulp_sensitivity_null_db = -52.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). On the 8-block test chain 54 of 63 mapped parameters moved the sound (ledger [2026-10-15T21:31:30+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L179 "smoke · native · smoke · mod_chain_repeat_null_db = -332.5 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). |
| Change models (swap an amp, cab or effect) | Yes. Any model or layout change is one whole write with read-back, never the pedal's own model command ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp T056 outcomes](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-t056-outcomes-lead "2026-10-15 (Stomp T056 outcomes, lead)")). 0.53 s per write. On firmware 3.80, one block from each of the six effect categories and two amps with impulse-response cabinets were built and read back (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). | Yes, from the block library: a model is loaded by its id from a stored template, with no new capture. Chains of several blocks still need a capture (0.56 s, once per chain). Mono only (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). |
| Switch blocks on and off by script | Yes. Bypass is toggled in place with a MIDI control (the delay's bypass moved with CC 110, ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)), and is part of every whole write. Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Yes. Each block's bypass is a MIDI control number in the captured template; one capture reproduces every bypass pattern almost exactly (-328.7 dB against files captured one by one, ledger [2026-10-15T21:31:30+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L179 "smoke · native · smoke · mod_chain_repeat_null_db = -332.5 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). Only chains captured since 2026-10-15 have it; older templates keep fixed bypass states ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, Native phase 2 block bypass](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-native-phase-2-block-bypass-over-midi-cc "2026-10-15 (native-engine, Native phase 2: block bypass over MIDI CC)")). |
| Two chains at once (throughput) | Path A and Path B give twice the renders per pass (measured on firmware 3.50, ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows); not re-measured on 3.80). The earlier gap of -18 to -22 dB between two-path and single-path renders was a layout bug, fixed by writing the layout the way the pedal's own editor stores it: on firmware 3.80 our two-path load now nulls against the editor-saved preset at -85.7 dB, equal to that preset's own repeat floor, against -9.3 dB before (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). A two-path pass also matches each chain rendered alone: Path A -72.9 dB (repeat floor -68.7), Path B -52.6 dB (floor -42.7). The rule that two-path renders are compared only with two-path renders was lifted on 2026-10-16 ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)")). Not tested: crossover and A/B split types; a pass with its own Path B input is refused by the compiler. Two-chain passes equal single-chain passes on firmware 3.80 for the tested chains (ledger [2026-10-16T15:44:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L595 "parity · stomp · twopath-parity-fw380 · null_residual_db = -72.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The code that renders two variants per pass was then compared on the pedal's audio against serial renders: path A/B nulls of -78.5/-60.4 and -85.1/-78.6 dB, each below the serial repeat floor, about 1.7 s per variant (ledger [2026-10-16T20:28:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L697 "parity · stomp · render-many-stomp-fw380 · null_dual_vs_serial_db_KA_pathA = -78.48 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows)). | Out of scope: two-path passes on Native will not be built (owner's decision, 2026-10-16); Native renders in parallel on 6 hosts instead. Block-serial chaining equals one instance at -67 to -79 dB above 20 Hz on the tested chains (ledger [2026-10-15T19:21:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L134 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · blockserial_null_db = -78.6 (first of 7 rows at this time)") (7 rows)). |
| Use the whole block and model catalog | In principle any compatible model can be written with no capture, because the pedal runs it itself. Tried so far: the amps, cabs, drives and delays in the test presets on firmware 3.50, and one block from each effect category and two amps with impulse-response cabinets on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Whether the 90 models gated by firmware now work on 3.80 has not been rechecked ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp firmware upgrade, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-firmware-upgrade-human "2026-10-15 (Stomp firmware upgrade, human)")). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Through the per-block library, now captured: 605 of the pedal's 607 models (the other 2, a transient shaper and a poly bass whammy, are refused by Native 3.82), each replayed in a fresh host (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Time-like and rate settings were re-captured with narrower control ranges (ledger [2026-10-16T16:04:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L638 "smoke · native · cc-range-v1 · delay_time_cc_step_ms_before = 62.99 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows), [2026-10-16T17:54:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L675 "smoke · native · cc-range-v2 · level_grid_null_db_worst = -136.4")). Not captured by design: routing models and the second cabinet of a dual-cab block. |
| Render speed | 3.49 s for a 2 s clip (the pedal plays the audio in real time); a 10 s clip is 10.7 s. Two-chain passes double throughput (see above). Measured on firmware 3.80: a 3 s clip takes 3.67 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:48:50+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L242 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · seconds_per_render_clip2s = 2.6889")). A search trial with the [T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base") speed options takes 3.76 s (render side only). See the speed-up row below for the search and verification gains. | 0.17 s for a 2 s clip and 1.24 s for a 10 s clip including 3 s of pre-roll. A pool of 6 parallel hosts (the approved cap) finishes a search test of a 2 s clip, render and score together, in 0.10 s, and one of a 10 s clip in 0.22 s (timing tables below). |
| Snapshots | Snapshot values are written into the document and must equal the block values ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 snapshot values, lead on stomp-engine T051](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-snapshot-values-lead-on-stomp-engine-t051 "2026-10-15 (snapshot values, lead on stomp-engine T051)")). Switching snapshots by MIDI (CC 69) recalls the stored values (ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)). Switching during a render is not tested. Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Native renders the block values and ignores snapshot values when a file is loaded (ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Snapshots are not switched in Native. |
| Agreement between the two engines | Measured on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-16T02:31:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L356 "parity · both · parity-spot-fw380 · null_residual_db = -37.45 (first of 15 rows at this time)") (15 rows), [2026-10-16T15:36:36+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L476 "parity · both · parity-timefx-stimuli-fw380 · rt60_max_abs_diff_pct = 6.494 (first of 10 rows at this time)") (10 rows), [2026-10-16T16:18:35+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L658 "parity · stomp · parity-timefx-stimuli-fw380 · sine330L_stomp_repeat_lfo_rate_diff_pct = -0.0756 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)): amps cancel at -37.5 to -47.5 dB and cabinets at -35.4 to -37.4 dB (pass line -30 dB); dynamics and equalisers pass. For delays, reverbs and modulation the comparison is by echo timing, decay and modulation rate rather than cancellation. Accepted result: Native stands in for amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, drives except Bitcrusher-type, delays, four reverb types, Hall above 500 Hz, and the modulation effects; the pedal still checks Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and cabinet Early Reflections. A suggested delay-time scaling of under 1 in 100 was rejected (it was Native's control step). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. (both engines) | |
| Reliability | The session-release lock is fixed ([T059](../../board/TASKS.md#t059 "Bug: pedal blocks stay locked after any control session")). Before the fix any control session left the pedal's block editing locked until USB was unplugged, even after the firmware 3.80 upgrade (ledger [2026-10-15T22:36:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L226 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_locked_after_tonepush_info = 1.0")). The helper now sends HX Edit's session close, and a person confirmed the blocks were editable afterwards (ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0")); the close was answered on every channel in a later smoke run (ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). One earlier control wedge that needed a power cycle is still unexplained ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 HX Stomp priorities, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-hx-stomp-priorities-human "2026-10-15 (HX Stomp priorities, human)")). Pedal verification on 2026-10-17 (ledger [2026-10-17T17:37:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L825 "smoke · stomp · stomp-verify-v1 · check1_setting_truth_pass = 20.0 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows)): setting truth 20 of 40, repeatability 12 of 17; the audio stream is unstable for about 0.7 s after start, amps carry state between renders, four models are refused, and a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. Pre-roll is now 1.0 s (2.0 s with an amp). No pedal result counts until that slip is handled. | Memory growth and crashes are fixed: the host stays at about 3.0 GB and repeats are identical to -313 dB (ledger [2026-10-15T18:54:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L127 "render-cost · native · smoke · seconds_per_render = 0.282 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)")). About 1 host process in 10 rendered every chain differently until a start-up check (the host canary) began replacing such hosts (ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)); Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history. Captures need a logged-in screen session. Verification on 2026-10-17 (ledger [2026-10-17T17:35:18+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L816 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · repeat_pass = 216.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)): repeat renders 216 of 216 at worst -161.5 dB after three engine fixes (baked controls no longer sent, hosts in the other numeric mode re-prepared, an amp state latch at open); setting truth 116 of 120 pairs (ledger [2026-10-17T17:38:13+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L831 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · truth_audit_pass = 116.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); a sentinel in the engine catches a forced drift (ledger [2026-10-17T17:29:36+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L812 "smoke · native · native-verify-v1 · sentinel_forced_drift_detected = 1.0")). Native results from before these fixes used the earlier engine. |
| Speed-ups since the last report | Measured on firmware 3.80 ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"), [docs/notes/stomp-speed.md](../../docs/notes/stomp-speed.md "HX Stomp speed (T061, stomp-engine, 2026-10-16, firmware 3.80)")): a search trial went from 10.861 s to 3.765 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows), 10 trials, 3 s clips, readback every 10 loads, measured tail waits). Per-test session overhead fell from 3.812 s to 0.772 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:47:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L240 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · session_overhead_per_test_old_s = 3.8123 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). A verification finalist (whole write plus two 10 s renders) improved only from 29.23 s to 24.65 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:10:35+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L272 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · verify_batch_per_finalist_s_before = 29.2271 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The shorter tail waits are only equal to the long waits with Hum and Ripple at 0 (ledger [2026-10-15T23:55:24+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L250 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · tail_wait_bound_s_Cdly0 = 4.809 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). The search now uses these options by default (task [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…")), but it has not been timed on the pedal. | Capture fell from about 31 s to 1.2 s per chain in one session, and 216 chains took 84.6 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L261 "render-cost · native · block-library-v0 · capture_per_chain_s = 31.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). A pool of 6 hosts ran 9.9 tests per second on 2 s clips and 4.6 on 10 s clips (ledger [2026-10-16T00:38:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L295 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 4.639 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)); 10 hosts ran 13.1 and 6.4 but with memory pressure and swapping, so the owner capped the pool at 6 on 2026-10-16. Newest batch capture: 391 single-block chains at 0.22 s each in 3 sessions with the parameter checks off (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). About 1 host in 10 rendered differently until a start-up check replaced it (ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). The timing tables below read these newer rows. |
| Platforms | Tested on macOS only. Linux is expected to work and Windows is unconfirmed; both are set aside for now ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 HX Stomp priorities, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-hx-stomp-priorities-human "2026-10-15 (HX Stomp priorities, human)")). The pedal is now on firmware 3.80: its smoke test and whole writes pass there (ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), but parity with Native and the firmware-gated models are not yet re-measured (spot checks [T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp") are next). | Tested on macOS with Native 3.82 as a VST3 plug-in in a helper process. Other platforms untested. Real MIDI control into the AU build did not change parameters ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, Native phase 2 block bypass](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-native-phase-2-block-bypass-over-midi-cc "2026-10-15 (native-engine, Native phase 2: block bypass over MIDI CC)")). |

### Estimated time for 100 tests

Each estimate is 100 times the measured time above. Where a speed-up from running things in parallel has been measured, the second column shows it (Native: several engine hosts at once; Stomp: two-path passes, one pass rendering both Path A and Path B), with the assumption it rests on listed below the table. Numbers marked "not measured" have no measurement behind them.

| Activity | Stomp, one after another | Stomp, two-path passes | Native, one after another | Native, in parallel | Both or neither, one after another |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| One render of a 2 s test clip | 5.8 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 2.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 16.8 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2) | 2.89 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2) | not measured |
| One render of a 10 s clip | 18 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 8.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 2.1 min (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 3) | 21.6 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 3) | not measured |
| One comparison (score) of a candidate against a target | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 5.35 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 4) |
| One parity comparison (per preset) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 47 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| One engine smoke test | 22 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | not measured |
| One search trial (render + score) | 6.4 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5, 1) | 3.2 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5, 1) | 58.7 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 6, 2) | 10.1 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 6, 2) | not measured |
| Verify one finalist on the pedal | 41 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured |
| A search run, per trial | 6.3 min (est., from 2026-10-16) | not measured | 84.6 s (est., from 2026-10-16) | not measured | not measured |
| One preset load or model change | 53.4 s (est., from 2026-10-15) | not measured | 15.0 s (est., from 2026-10-14) | not measured | not measured |
| First-time capture of a new chain | not measured | not measured | 56.3 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 7) | not measured | not measured |
| One probe analysis (offline) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| **Combined: testing** | 22 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 1.3 h (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| **Combined: comparison** | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 5.35 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 4) |
| **Combined: modelling** | 6.8 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 3.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 78.4 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2, 7) | 64.5 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2, 7) | not measured |

Assumptions behind the estimates:

1. Stomp, in parallel means two-path passes: one pass renders two chains at once (Path A + B), so the throughput doubles. The gain of 2.0 was measured on fw 3.50; the dual-path-only comparison rule was lifted on 2026-10-16 after the [T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80") layout fix (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), but the gain is not re-measured on fw 3.80
2. Native: 6 engine hosts at once (a render pool; the human approved a cap of 6 hosts on 2026-10-16), speed-up measured with 2 s clips as tests per second
3. Native: 6 engine hosts at once (a render pool; the human approved a cap of 6 hosts on 2026-10-16), speed-up measured with 10 s clips as tests per second
4. One after another: no parallel speed-up is measured for this activity (the benchmark steps run in sequence and a parity check needs the single pedal)
5. Stomp: one engine session serves the whole batch, so the session overhead is paid once, not once per test ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"))
6. Native: a search test is a render plus its score; the pool figures count both together (tests per second, 3 s pre-roll included), so no separate scoring time is added
7. Native captures are counted one after another at the per-chain time in the newest ledger row

Typical sizes come from the repository (cited in each row); times are those sizes multiplied by the measured per-unit times above. Estimates, not measurements, and they regenerate from the ledger and notes every run.

| Capture the library with 8 blocks per capture: The same library, if one capture carries up to 8 blocks. | Native | 83 captures if 8 blocks share a capture (source: [docs/DECISIONS.md](../../docs/DECISIONS.md "Decision log") (block bypass over MIDI CC: up to 8 blocks per capture)) | 0.56 s per capture | 46.7 s (est., from 2026-10-16) | not measured | capturing chains (46.7 s); proposed packing, not yet used for the library |
| Probe one block at one setting (Native): Fingerprint one block: frequency response, distortion and dynamics at one setting. | Native | 1 pass of 47.25 s of audio per settings point, number of settings points not yet defined (source: [docs/notes/block-probe.md](../../docs/notes/block-probe.md "block-probe (T046, offline part)") (duration)) | estimated 4.80 s per pass: the measured 10 s render scaled by audio length | 4.80 s (est., from 2026-10-16) | 0.83 s (est., from 2026-10-16); 6 hosts | rendering (4.80 s); per settings point; a probe pass through an engine is not measured |
| Probe every block at one setting (Native): A fingerprint of the whole library at one setting each. | Native | 661 blocks x 1 settings point (illustration: the real grid is not defined) (source: [board/TASKS.md](../../board/TASKS.md "Task board") [T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order") and [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)")) | 4.80 s per pass | 53 min (est., from 2026-10-16) | 9.2 min (est., from 2026-10-16); 6 hosts | rendering (53 min); multiply by the number of settings points |
| Probe one block at one setting (Stomp): Compare the pedal against Native block by block. | Stomp | 1 pass per settings point (source: [docs/notes/block-probe.md](../../docs/notes/block-probe.md "block-probe (T046, offline part)")) | no Stomp render of a long clip is measured | not measured | not measured | not measured |

**Takeaway.** The longest job today, one after another, is "Synthetic benchmark, Stomp recovery on all 60 items" at 9.5 h, most of it rendering (9.4 h); next is "Synthetic benchmark, full recovery (Native)" at 2.9 h. Parallel Native hosts cut "Synthetic benchmark, full recovery (Native)" from 2.9 h to 30 min; the Stomp has no such speed-up beyond two chains per pass. For Native, capturing is no longer the slow step when chains are captured in a batch: 0.56 s per chain in the newest row, against 1.24 s for a 10 s test; a single capture that includes engine start-up costs far more (see the measured table). 1 job has no measurement yet and shows "not measured".

#### Helix Native engine

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Render a DI clip through a candidate preset offline in Helix Native, fast enough to be the searching engine.  
**Done when.** A NativeEngine behind the frozen interface renders any chain structure the search needs at a measured cost, loads candidates without a GUI step per candidate, agrees with the Stomp within a measured null residual, and has run a full synthetic recovery pass.  
**Functionality today.** Loads an arbitrary generated .hlx into Native with no human (in-process file drop, about 66 s per new chain structure, once per machine; needs TONEMATCH_NATIVE_AUTOCAPTURE=1 and a logged-in GUI session), drives parameters through a MIDI-CC map, and renders a 2 s clip in about 0.28 s with memory flat near 3.0 GB. Renders are history-independent and match each file's own capture at -317 dB. Mono in and out only; chains with an enabled delay or reverb need a 3 s pre-roll; Captures of new single-block chains take 0.22 s each when batched across three sessions with the parameter checks off (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), and a pool of 6 hosts renders and scores about 10 two-second tests per second. Windows and Linux are untested. Single-block templates for 605 pedal models load by model id with no capture (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Verified on 2026-10-17: settings land exactly apart from recorded misses, and renders repeat to -161.5 dB.  
**Progress note.** Hands-off loading (file drop plus MIDI-CC map), state-based reset renders (0.28 s, bit-identical to dedicated captures at -317 dB, flat 3.0 GB), stale-template protection and a memory watchdog are built, hardware-measured on one Mac, and exercised by a 449-render real-song search. Block-serial rendering is shown to equal one instance above 20 Hz (-67 to -79 dB). Still missing: Windows and Linux (the drop route is macOS VST3 only), stereo and dual-cab paths in the host, probe fingerprints, and any full synthetic recovery pass. Quality of what Native renders versus the pedal is only partly in parity (see evaluation), and nothing it renders has been blind-tested by ear. Phase 3 is finished ([T055](../../board/TASKS.md#t055 "Native phase 3: parallel render pool for search"), [T064](../../board/TASKS.md#t064 "Native phase 3 follow-ups")): capture takes 1.2 s per chain in one session and 0.39 s with three, instead of 31 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L261 "render-cost · native · block-library-v0 · capture_per_chain_s = 31.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); a render pool of 6 hosts (the cap the human approved on 2026-10-16) runs 9.9 two-second tests per second and 4.6 ten-second tests per second, each test being a render plus its score (ledger [2026-10-16T00:38:02+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L294 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 9.879")); block bypass by CC matches separate captures at -328.7 dB (ledger [2026-10-16T00:29:34+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L285 "parity · native · smoke · bypass_patterns_cc_vs_drop_truth_db = -328.7")); a rejected model load is detected and closed; about 1 host in 10 that rendered differently is replaced by a start-up check; templates B and B0 are re-captured at the frozen values (ledger [2026-10-16T00:50:49+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L300 "render-cost · native · templates-B-B0 · capture_per_chain_s = 9.51 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)); and a scan of 607 Stomp block models found 2 that Native 3.82 refuses (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The block library is now captured: 605 of 605 Stomp models (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)); delay times move in 10 ms steps because Native honours a narrowed CC range (ledger [2026-10-16T16:04:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L638 "smoke · native · cc-range-v1 · delay_time_cc_step_ms_before = 62.99 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), and Block Level has a finer range (ledger [2026-10-16T17:54:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L675 "smoke · native · cc-range-v2 · level_grid_null_db_worst = -136.4")). Written but not yet committed: render_many on the pool, a shared time-variant flag and small fixes; block fingerprints ([T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)")) are in progress. Measured on one Mac only; nothing here has been checked by ear. Results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history (human decision). 2026-10-17: native-verify-v1 ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")) first results: baked-value violations 0 (ledger [2026-10-17T16:29:41+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L743 "smoke · native · native-verify-v1 · baked_violations = 0.0")); repeatability 215 of 216 renders within the line, worst -97.4 dB against -100 dB, so that check has not passed as registered (ledger [2026-10-17T16:34:08+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L744 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · repeat_pass = 215.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Fingerprints exist for 602 models (ledger [2026-10-16T19:13:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L692 "smoke · native · render-many-v1 · render_many_vs_serial_null_db_worst = -306.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). E3 (out-of-range values) and E6 (real-guitar smoke DI) are in progress; on-demand capture defaults to off. 2026-10-17 ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")): verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"). Three engine fixes: baked controls are not sent, hosts that open in the other numeric mode are re-prepared in place, and the first amp loaded is latched at open. Repeatability 216 of 216; an in-run sentinel exists; unverifiable models are excluded from search pools; on-demand capture defaults off.  
**Board tasks.** 17 of 19 done, 2 in progress: [T002](../../board/TASKS.md#t002 "Spike: can pedalboard write a whole preset into Helix Native's saved…"), [T012](../../board/TASKS.md#t012 "Install Helix Native 3.8x and HX Edit 3.82"), [T013](../../board/TASKS.md#t013 "Run scripts/native_spike.py --gui --golden .."), [T018n](../../board/TASKS.md#t018n "Hands-off Native loading"), [T026](../../board/TASKS.md#t026 "Explain and re-measure the Native render-time gap"), [T043](../../board/TASKS.md#t043 "Native host memory (human report"), [T044](../../board/TASKS.md#t044 "Native host memory: find and fix the growth"), [T045](../../board/TASKS.md#t045 "Spike: block-serial rendering — one block per Native instance chained…"), [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)"), [T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order"), [T055](../../board/TASKS.md#t055 "Native phase 3: parallel render pool for search"), [T064](../../board/TASKS.md#t064 "Native phase 3 follow-ups"), [T072](../../board/TASKS.md#t072 "Narrower CC ranges for time-like and rate parameters in Native…"), [T080](../../board/TASKS.md#t080 "N3 engine push: NativeEngine.render_many(dry, variants) on…"), [T081](../../board/TASKS.md#t081 "N4 engine push: native_library.is_time_variant(model_id)"), [T082](../../board/TASKS.md#t082 "N6 engine push: (a) exact 0.0 command lands at lo,"), [T098](../../board/TASKS.md#t098 "E3 engine push: values outside the narrowed CC ranges load…"), [T099](../../board/TASKS.md#t099 "E6 engine push: engine-smoke uses the licence-clean real DI…"), [T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")

**Challenges and findings**

- Host memory grew 4 to 11 GB and was killed: pedalboard treats Native as keeping audio on reset, so every reset render re-created the plugin and Native never freed an instance (+353 MB each). Fixed by resetting by state; renders are now 0.28 s instead of about 2 s and bit-identical to dedicated captures. Scores from runs before 2026-10-15T18:54 are not exactly reproducible (history-dependent at -62 to -72 dB). _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)"); ledger [2026-10-15T18:54:10](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L127 "render-cost · native · smoke · seconds_per_render = 0.282 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)]_
- The old and new reset paths disagree on randomly set parameters (levels 11.6 to 14.4 dB apart). The new path matches each file's own drop capture, so it is believed correct, but this is not independently confirmed. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)") (open question)]_
- A stale template was reused for a second file with the same chain structure but a different unmapped parameter, which produced a false parity row (voided). Templates are now keyed by structure plus a hash of baked values and legacy templates are refused. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stale Native template](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stale-native-template-lead "2026-10-15 (stale Native template, lead)"); [ledger/void.csv](../../ledger/void.csv "ledger/void.csv")]_
- SUPERSEDED 2026-10-16 ([T082](../../board/TASKS.md#t082 "N6 engine push: (a) exact 0.0 command lands at lo,"): the host's state reset clears every tail at any pre-roll, -291 to -316 dB; DECISIONS): Native's reset does not clear delay lines: a chain with an enabled delay alternates between two renders (-19.7 dB) at the default 0.1 s pre-roll; 3 s fixes it (-62 to -65 dB). _[ledger [2026-10-15T00:16:00+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L91 "render-cost · native · smoke · autocapture_s = 27.9 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)]_
- Block-serial: the -22.6 dB untrimmed result is an artefact of each instance's 16-sample delay against modulation; trimming 16 samples per instance gives -67.1 dB above 20 Hz, but the full band is limited to -50 dB by sub-5 Hz content from the delay. An Amp+Cab block is not exactly an amp block followed by a cab block (0.021 dB, -72.7 dB gain-matched). An unexplained -67 dB floor appears once the amp follows other blocks. Dual cab, stereo paths and non-default settings are not tested. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T045](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t045-block-serial-spike "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T045 block-serial spike)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:21:10](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L134 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · blockserial_null_db = -78.6 (first of 7 rows at this time)") (7 rows) blockserial-v0]_
- Native applies a drop-loaded file's block values and ignores its stale snapshot values (-330.1 dB versus -38.3 dB); capture now syncs snapshots to blocks. A block-bypass MIDI-CC encoding Native honours has not been found, so bypass patterns need one capture each. _[ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 T018n](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-native-engine-t018n-hands-off-native-loading-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (native-engine, T018n hands-off Native loading; proposals for the lead)") stage 2]_
- Whole-preset state write is impossible (opaque encrypted records); the file drop is the supported route. The human clicked 'Change HC mode' once and ticked 'Don't ask again' to get a clean start state; no state edits were adopted. _[[docs/notes/native-spike.md](../../docs/notes/native-spike.md "T002 / T013 Native spike: can a whole preset be written into Helix Native's state?"); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 human rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-human-rulings-on-native-recorded-by-lead "2026-10-14 (human rulings on Native, recorded by lead)")]_
- The Native installer EULA is silent on training ML on Native renders; the human marked the EULA reading done but recorded no finding. _[[docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md](../../docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md "Line 6 EULA flags for automation and protocol use (not legal advice)"); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 project licence](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-project-licence-lead-at-the-humans-request "2026-10-14 (project licence, lead, at the human's request)")]_
- About 1 Native host process in 10 rendered every chain differently for its whole life (reverbs and modulated delays by -21 to -27 dB; parameters and saved state identical; cause unknown). A start-up check now replaces such hosts. Latency trims could also differ by up to 30 samples between engines; fixed by measuring once per engine with every block bypassed. _[ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-16 native-engine Native phase 3](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-native-engine-t064-native-phase-3-follow-ups-for-the-lead-search-benchmark "2026-10-16 (native-engine, T064 Native phase 3 follow-ups; for the lead, search, benchmark)")]_

**Still to do**

- Stereo-capable host path (the host renders mono in and folded mono out) before stereo blocks can be serialised
- Find a block-bypass CC encoding Native honours, or extend the template key with the enabled pattern
- Linux via Wine and yabridge and Windows are untested
- [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)") block-probe fingerprints in Native; [T080](../../board/TASKS.md#t080 "N3 engine push: NativeEngine.render_many(dry, variants) on…") render_many on the pool; [T081](../../board/TASKS.md#t081 "N4 engine push: native_library.is_time_variant(model_id)") shared time-variant flag; [T082](../../board/TASKS.md#t082 "N6 engine push: (a) exact 0.0 command lands at lo,") small items (committed by the lead after review)

**Open blockers**

- Misses recorded as rule failures (7 one-step rounding landings, 7 input/output blocks) are explained and do not block [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)").

**Decisions**

- Native route: file drop plus MIDI-CC map, autocapture default off ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 human rulings on Native](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-human-rulings-on-native-recorded-by-lead "2026-10-14 (human rulings on Native, recorded by lead)"))
- pedalboard (GPLv3) stays behind a process boundary ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)"))
- Host resets by state, memory watchdog at 5120 MB ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)"))
- Block-serial corrections: trim 16 samples per instance, judge above 20 Hz, store amps and cabs as separate blocks ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T045](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t045-block-serial-spike "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T045 block-serial spike)"))
- 3 s pre-roll for chains with delay or reverb ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15, lead](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-pedal-lockups-lead "2026-10-15 (pedal lockups, lead)"))
- Native host cap is 6; Hum and Ripple frozen at 0 on both engines; Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 human, Native phase 3 follow-ups](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-human-recorded-by-tone-matcher-lead-native-phase-3-follow-ups-and-humripple "2026-10-16 (human, recorded by tone-matcher-lead): Native phase 3 follow-ups and Hum/Ripple"))
- Two-path passes on Native are out of scope (human, DECISIONS 2026-10-16)
- Block Level CC range \[-25.75, +6\] dB and optional Engine.render_many (lead, DECISIONS 2026-10-16)

**Risks**

- PLAN risk: Native EULA silent on ML training: findings of the human's EULA reading not recorded
- PLAN risk: Native on Linux and Windows (the drop route is macOS only)
- Native and Stomp differ in at least one parameter (cab Early Reflections); see evaluation
- Scores and parity rows from before the host-reset fix and the 2026-10-16 host canary are not exactly reproducible (about 1 host in 10 rendered differently); they stay as history and the ones we rely on are re-measured
- Native does not stand in for Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives or cabinet Early Reflections ([T066b](../../board/TASKS.md#t066b "Parity probe session closing the T066 open questions") ruling)

#### HX Stomp engine and hardware reamp

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Drive the real pedal from code and capture its USB audio, so the final check is the actual hardware and hardware-only users have an engine.  
**Done when.** A StompEngine renders any candidate within the pedal's capabilities on macOS, Windows and Linux, with edit-buffer-only control, a calibrated reamp path, parity to Native measured, and a user-DI accuracy mode used on real songs.  
**Functionality today.** Writes a whole generated preset into the pedal's edit buffer (about 0.3 s), reads back every parameter and refuses to render a mismatch, reamps a DI over USB 5/6 to 1/2 (2.7 s for a 2 s clip, 3.7 s for 3 s), ends each session with HX Edit's session close so the pedal's blocks stay editable, and restores the original preset byte for byte. Verified on firmware 3.80 and macOS only; whole_preset is off by default; layout changes in place are unsupported; two-path (Path A plus B) loads use the editor's own layout and match single-chain renders (Path A -72.9 dB against a repeat floor of -68.7, Path B -52.6 against -42.7; ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), with the 2.0x two-chains-per-pass gain measured on firmware 3.50 only.  
**Progress note.** Control, reamp, latency, restore and whole-preset injection with a parameter-by-parameter readback on every load are verified on one pedal and one OS (macOS), now on firmware 3.80 (the pedal was upgraded on 2026-10-15). The session-release lock is fixed: the helper now ends a session the way HX Edit does, and blocks stay editable (ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0"), [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The engine smoke test passes on 3.80, 9 of 9 whole-preset writes landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Speed work ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base")): a search trial went from 10.9 s to 3.8 s and per-test session overhead from 3.81 s to 0.77 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows), [2026-10-15T23:47:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L240 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · session_overhead_per_test_old_s = 3.8123 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The two-path layout difference is fixed ([T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80")): our two-path load of a stored preset nulled at -9.3 dB against the editor-saved one and now nulls at -85.7 dB, equal to that preset's own repeat floor (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); single-path loads are unchanged. Hum and Ripple are frozen at 0 on both engines (human decision, 2026-10-16). Still missing: Windows and Linux, the play-along path on hardware, the whole_preset default (still off), the Early Reflections mismatch with Native, and any speed timing of the search that now uses the options. Parity with Native on 3.80 has not been run. 2026-10-16: two-path passes equal single-path passes on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-16T15:44:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L595 "parity · stomp · twopath-parity-fw380 · null_residual_db = -72.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Engine push [T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check")-[T095](../../board/TASKS.md#t095 "Engine-push pedal session"): a shared pedal lock with a device check ([T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check")), two renders per pass ([T091](../../board/TASKS.md#t091 "S1 engine push: StompEngine.render_many")), layout-changing loads written whole ([T092](../../board/TASKS.md#t092 "S2 engine push: layout-changing loads through the whole-preset…")), the Simple Delay Scale report explained ([T093](../../board/TASKS.md#t093 "S4 engine push: Simple Delay Scale reported missing on 11C")) and a whole-preset default proposal ([T094](../../board/TASKS.md#t094 "S5 engine push: whole_preset default proposal")) are written; the control-only pedal session passed and restored 04C, but every CoreAudio stream failed so the audio comparison did not run (ledger [2026-10-16T18:30:48+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L688 "smoke · stomp · smoke · restore_bytes_equal = 1.0 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Not all of it is committed. 2026-10-16/17: the two-variant pass equals serial renders on the pedal (ledger [2026-10-16T20:28:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L697 "parity · stomp · render-many-stomp-fw380 · null_dual_vs_serial_db_KA_pathA = -78.48 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows), about 1.7 s per variant) and restores are judged by decoded equality ([T097](../../board/TASKS.md#t097 "E4: judge pedal restores by decoded equality"), ledger [2026-10-17T00:31:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L742 "smoke · stomp · smoke · restore_decoded_equal = 1.0")). Pedal-side verification (stomp-verify-v1) is running; it has no ledger row yet. 2026-10-17 ([T108](../../board/TASKS.md#t108 "HX Stomp verification suite scripts/stomp_verify.py")): the pedal verification found real defects: the USB audio stream is not stable for about 0.7 s after start, amps carry state from the previous render, four models are refused in whole-preset writes, some time parameters glide slowly in place, and a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. Pre-roll is now 1.0 s (2.0 s with an amp). No pedal result counts until [T112](../../board/TASKS.md#t112 "Residual one-sample USB slips") lands. The human confirmed the pedal blocks are editable.  
**Board tasks.** 18 of 22 done, 4 in progress: [T003](../../board/TASKS.md#t003 "Spike: fretwire on Linux drives a block, model and parameter change"), [T004](../../board/TASKS.md#t004 "Measure USB round-trip latency with an impulse"), [T014](../../board/TASKS.md#t014 "Pedal batch H1-H11 from docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md"), [T018s](../../board/TASKS.md#t018s "Spike: autonomous whole-preset injection on the HX Stomp"), [T019](../../board/TASKS.md#t019 "Own Stomp engine-smoke row once the human approves running the local…"), [T020a](../../board/TASKS.md#t020a "Stomp firmware 3.50 vs 3.80"), [T042](../../board/TASKS.md#t042 "Play-along user-DI capture"), [T051](../../board/TASKS.md#t051 "Bug: rank2_H_live delay read back Mix 0.72 / Feedback 0.52 / Time…"), [T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"), [T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80"), [T071](../../board/TASKS.md#t071 "Two-path parity on the Stomp"), [T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check"), [T091](../../board/TASKS.md#t091 "S1 engine push: StompEngine.render_many"), [T092](../../board/TASKS.md#t092 "S2 engine push: layout-changing loads through the whole-preset…"), [T093](../../board/TASKS.md#t093 "S4 engine push: Simple Delay Scale reported missing on 11C"), [T094](../../board/TASKS.md#t094 "S5 engine push: whole_preset default proposal"), [T095](../../board/TASKS.md#t095 "Engine-push pedal session"), [T097](../../board/TASKS.md#t097 "E4: judge pedal restores by decoded equality"), [T108](../../board/TASKS.md#t108 "HX Stomp verification suite scripts/stomp_verify.py"), [T110](../../board/TASKS.md#t110 "Device refuses whole documents of VIC Stutter Edit, L6SPB Inf…"), [T111](../../board/TASKS.md#t111 "In-place time-parameter glide"), [T112](../../board/TASKS.md#t112 "Residual one-sample USB slips")

**Challenges and findings**

- The pedal's blocks stayed locked after any control session (ours and unmodified upstream tonepush, on firmware 3.50 and 3.80). Cause: no HX Edit-style session close. Fixed in the helper; blocks editable afterwards, close acknowledged on control and data channels (events not acknowledged), no USB reset needed. _[ledger [2026-10-15T22:36:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L226 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_locked_after_tonepush_info = 1.0") (still locked on 3.80 before the fix); ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0"); ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine T059](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-engine-t059-session-release-phases-1-and-2-for-the-lead "2026-10-15 (stomp-engine, T059 session release, phases 1 and 2; for the lead)")]_
- The pedal recalls the current snapshot's controller values over the block values on load. The compiler used to keep the base document's snapshot values, so a generated preset's delay ran Feedback 0.52 instead of 0.40 and the human found it 'way too much' live. Fixed in the compiler (ledger 19:36:29); every load is now verified. _[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine T051; ledger [2026-10-15T19:20:34](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L133 "ab · stomp · DS-Sample · live_pref_delay_mix = 0.1")]_
- Delay Mix 0.72 and Time 0.47 read back from the pedal are in neither the file nor the base document. Unexplained; the hardware re-check after the fix is pending. The only results affected are the live listening session and the never-run H parity. _[board [T051](../../board/TASKS.md#t051 "Bug: rank2_H_live delay read back Mix 0.72 / Feedback 0.52 / Time…"); DECISIONS 2026-10-15]_
- Whole-preset injection works with audio (4 of 4), but 2 of 4 pre-registered predictions were wrong or inconclusive (crest wrong, delay tail inconclusive) and the first render of one preset was an outlier (-27.9 versus -40.3 dB), so whole_preset stays off. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 T018s rendered](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t018s-rendered-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T018s rendered; for the lead)"); ledger 2026-10-14T23:47]_
- Terminal needs Microphone permission for reamping: without it captures are digital zero. HX Edit holds the control interface exclusively and must be quit during runs. _[[docs/DEV-ENV.md](../../docs/DEV-ENV.md "Dev environment notes"); [docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md](../../docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md "T003 spike: driving the HX Stomp from code (stomp-engine, 2026-10-13)")]_
- Hardware rendering is serial and real time, so recovery over 60 items is about 10 h and a CI run on hardware is not practical. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- stompd source is intentionally not in the repo, so Stomp results cannot be reproduced by others yet. _[[docs/notes/stompd.md](../../docs/notes/stompd.md "stompd: the HX Stomp control helper (stomp-engine, 2026-10-14)")]_
- With amp Hum and Ripple free, two Stomp renders of the same chain differ at about -30 dB (the hum runs free); with both at 0 the repeats are -72 to -91 dB. Tail-wait shortening is only safe with both at 0. Decided 2026-10-16: both are frozen at 0 on both engines. _[ledger [2026-10-15T23:55:24+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L250 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · tail_wait_bound_s_Cdly0 = 4.809 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows); [docs/notes/stomp-speed.md](../../docs/notes/stomp-speed.md "HX Stomp speed (T061, stomp-engine, 2026-10-16, firmware 3.80)")]_
- Two-path presets rendered -18 to -22 dB away from single-path renders. Cause: the compiler kept the single-path base's junctions (Balance/Pan 0/1, attach points 0/0, topology code 0), so the pedal re-derived a different layout. Fixed by writing HX Edit's layout; now at the editor preset's own repeat floor. _[ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine T063](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)")]_

**Decisions**

- macOS control route: USB interface 0 via stompd behind the JSON-lines protocol ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 stomp-engine](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t018s-rendered-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T018s rendered; for the lead)"))
- Edit buffer only, no flash writes (same entry)
- Block values win in every generated preset; every load is verified by readback ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T051 and snapshot values](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t051-snapshot-values-in-native "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T051 snapshot values in Native)"))
- Firmware 3.80 is the pedal's firmware since 2026-10-15; everything measured on 3.50 is rechecked as it is used ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp firmware upgrade](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-firmware-upgrade-human "2026-10-15 (Stomp firmware upgrade, human)"))
- reamp_capture --user-di only reads the pedal ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 user-DI capture](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-user-di-capture-lead "2026-10-15 (user-DI capture, lead)"))
- Hum and Ripple frozen at 0 on both engines (DECISIONS 2026-10-16 human)
- The dual-path-only comparison rule is lifted: two-path passes may be used for checks ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout, lead](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)"))

**Risks**

- Pedal control unavailable on Windows (macOS confirmed, Windows unverified)
- Native and Stomp differ in cab Early Reflections (parity)
- Stomp results not reproducible by others while stompd is unpublished
- Unexplained delay readback values on the pedal

#### Engine interface and selection

**Status:** working · **Health:** ok · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** One interface (load, set_params, render, cost, capabilities, close) so every other step is engine-agnostic, plus the rule for which engine runs.  
**Done when.** The interface is frozen, both engines implement it, and the auto selection (Native searches, Stomp verifies) runs a real search and verification pass in one session.  
**Functionality today.** Both engines implement the frozen interface; TONEMATCH_ENGINE=native\|stomp\|auto selects one; engine-smoke detects an engine, renders a clip and checks level, silence, NaN and length.  
**Progress note.** The contract is frozen (2026-10-14) and implemented twice, with a registry and a smoke script. render_many was added as an optional method on 2026-10-16 (contract amendment; Native pool and Stomp two-path implementations), and the auto role split has not been exercised by a search-then-verify run.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T001](../../board/TASKS.md#t001 "Freeze engine interface v0"), [T008](../../board/TASKS.md#t008 "engine-smoke script and the first ledger row"), [T015](../../board/TASKS.md#t015 "Re-run engine-smoke on each engine once one is present")  
**Owners.** tone-matcher-lead, native-engine, stomp-engine, benchmark

**Implemented**

- Engine abstract base class and capability and cost types [`src/tonematch/engines/base.py`](../../src/tonematch/engines/base.py "Engine interface, v0. The spec is contracts/engine-interface.md; this is its code form.")
- Engine detection and role-based selection [`src/tonematch/engines/registry.py`](../../src/tonematch/engines/registry.py "Engine detection and selection. TONEMATCH_ENGINE=native\|stomp\|auto (default auto).")
- engine-smoke script with a restore check [`scripts/engine_smoke.py`](../../scripts/engine_smoke.py "engine-smoke: detect engines, load a known preset, render the 2 s DI, check, write ledger rows.")
- Frozen spec with resolved Native and Stomp routes [`contracts/engine-interface.md`](../../contracts/engine-interface.md "Engine interface, v0 (frozen 2026-10-14, Gate 1)")

**Decisions**

- Engine interface v0 frozen ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 Gate 1 prep](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-gate-1-prep-lead "2026-10-14 (Gate 1 prep, lead)"))
- Engine is user-selectable; at least one required ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- Edit-buffer-only rule ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)"))

**Risks**

- Native and Stomp differ: low to medium (PLAN risk table)

#### Preset generation and validation (.hlx, catalog import)

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Represent, write and validate a Line 6 .hlx preset that HX Edit accepts and the pedal loads, using a model catalog read at runtime from the user's own install.  
**Done when.** Search results become a valid .hlx (full-rig and effects-only, up to three snapshots, DSP budget and amp/cab pairing respected) that imports into HX Edit and loads on the pedal, for every block the search can choose.  
**Functionality today.** Reads and writes .hlx byte-equal for both golden templates, imports the 677-model catalog from HX Edit 3.82, validates blocks, DSP cost, pairing and device version, and exports full-rig or effects-only; does not yet build snapshots from a match. Generated files are not yet forced to carry snapshot values equal to their blocks (the check lives in the parity script and the engines). 2026-10-17 ([T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")): the curated corpus (2,068 presets after cleaning) was analysed for category order, super-groups, usage concentration and metadata strength; only category-level statistics are committed.  
**Progress note.** Parse, byte-equal round trip, catalog import, validation and both export modes exist and one generated file was accepted by HX Edit and the pedal. Snapshot generation from a search result, the schema extensions, and the DSP unit and budget semantics are unverified or not built. [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder") (2026-10-17): chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only, data/chain_prior/category_prior.json).  
**Board tasks.** 5 of 5 done: [T005](../../board/TASKS.md#t005 "catalog-import: locate the user's HX Edit install on each OS, read…"), [T006](../../board/TASKS.md#t006 ".hlx round-trip: parse a golden template into Preset, write it back…"), [T010](../../board/TASKS.md#t010 "Confirm Stomp firmware 3.80 and Native 3.82"), [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder"), [T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")  
**Owners.** preset

**Challenges and findings**

- The Stomp golden says device_version 3.50 and the Native golden 3.80; generated files must match the pedal's firmware or HX Edit refuses them (error -8309 means wrong device). _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 2](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-2-mode-1 "2026-10-14 (cycle 2, mode 1)"); [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") prep]_
- known.hlx has been accepted, but only one generated file has ever gone through HX Edit and the pedal; the two ledger rows are human-reported, not observed by benchmark. _[[board/GATES.md](../../board/GATES.md "Gates") [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)")]_
- Slots follow real @position order, not a fixed 8-slot template; the catalog's per-model load seeds a DSP cost table whose units and budget are unverified. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 2](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-2-mode-1 "2026-10-14 (cycle 2, mode 1)")]_
- A file whose snapshots disagree with its blocks plays the snapshot values on the pedal but the block values in Native; one earlier generated file had this and a live session heard the difference. parity_null.py check now refuses such files, but the preset writers themselves do not yet enforce the rule. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 snapshot values](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t051-snapshot-values-in-native "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T051 snapshot values in Native)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)]_

**Still to do**

- Put the proposed schema extensions into [contracts/preset-schema.md](../../contracts/preset-schema.md "Preset schema, v0") at the next contract pass
- Snapshot builder: up to three snapshots from section groups with the model-sharing rule
- Verify the DSP budget on the pedal
- hlx-validate on a preset built from a search result
- Move the snapshot-sync helpers (currently in engines/native.py) into tonematch.preset and call them in write_hlx (proposal open)

**Open blockers**

- Snapshot generation and the proposed schema extensions are not in the contract yet; DSP budget units are unverified.

**Decisions**

- Catalog source is the per-model definition files in the HX Edit Resources folder, not the names-only catalog JSON ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- Two export modes ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- Schema extensions and DSP-cost table proposals ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1 and 2026-10-14 cycle](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)") 2)
- Block values win in every generated preset; snapshot controller values equal the block values ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 snapshot values, lead](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-snapshot-values-lead-on-stomp-engine-t051 "2026-10-15 (snapshot values, lead on stomp-engine T051)"))

#### Guitar separation and section segmentation

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, real-songs

**Purpose.** Isolate the guitar from a mix, say how reliable the isolation is, and cluster the song into at most three tone groups for snapshots.  
**Done when.** On a stress set of real songs the separator's guitar stem is good enough that the loss still ranks the true tone first (a curve of match quality against separation quality), the confidence estimate is calibrated, and segmentation agrees with a human's section labels.  
**Functionality today.** Separates a song with htdemucs_6s (7.8 s warm on MPS, 13.1 s on CPU for 32 s), accepts user-supplied stems, segments into sections and at most three tone groups, and computes a heuristic confidence mask; nothing here is shown to work on a real recording.  
**Progress note.** htdemucs_6s runs on MPS and CPU and the pieces exist, but the only quality number is an SDR of 0.002 dB on a synthetic Karplus-Strong mix where the guitar went to the wrong stem, so real-song quality is unmeasured. Confidence is an uncalibrated heuristic, segmentation is unvalidated, and the RoFormer path was never run. On the first real song the htdemucs_6s guitar stem and its per-sample confidence (0.994 on the chosen window) were used for the target; no ground truth exists, so quality remains unmeasured.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T020](../../board/TASKS.md#t020 "separate skill with backend detection and htdemucs_6s first"), [T020b](../../board/TASKS.md#t020b "RoFormer adapter licence read + install"), [T070](../../board/TASKS.md#t070 "Separation stress curve sep-stress-v1")  
**Owners.** separation

**Implemented**

- separate() with backend detection (CUDA, MPS, ROCm, DirectML, CPU) and a cache keyed by song hash [`src/tonematch/separation/core.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/core.py "separate() and recombine_and_reseparate().") [`src/tonematch/separation/models.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/models.py "Pluggable separators. `htdemucs_6s` is the default; RoFormer checkpoints sit behind a flag.") [`src/tonematch/separation/stems.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/stems.py "Stems container and the on-disk cache layout (cache/stems/<song-hash>/<model>/).") [`scripts/separate.py`](../../scripts/separate.py "separate (owner: separation). See .claude/skills/separate/SKILL.md.") [`src/tonematch/gpu.py`](../../src/tonematch/gpu.py "Compute backend detection. Stub: no hard dependency on torch or onnxruntime.")
- Section segmentation, tone clustering to at most 3 groups, confidence mask, recombine-and-re-separate comparison [`src/tonematch/separation/segment.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/segment.py "Beats, bars, sections and snapshot grouping (first cut; constant-tempo, 4/4 assumed).") [`src/tonematch/separation/confidence.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/confidence.py "Artifact-confidence masks (time-frequency), a heuristic proxy, not a calibrated probability.")
- Synthetic mix generator for tests [`src/tonematch/separation/synth.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/synth.py "Short synthetic mixes with known stems, for tests and the separate smoke run. No real audio.")
