**Challenges and findings**

- On the synthetic mix htdemucs_6s put the guitar into the 'other' stem (SDR about 0 dB), so a clean-synthetic test says nothing about real songs. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:20:57+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L32 "separate · - · synthetic-sep-v0 · guitar_sdr_db = 0.002")]_
- The only guitar-capable catalogue checkpoint (BS-Roformer-SW) has no stated licence and an unverifiable origin; it is gated behind TONEMATCH_ROFORMER_LICENCE_ACK=1 and nothing was downloaded. mel-roformer and mdx23c have no guitar-capable checkpoint. _[[docs/notes/separation-models.md](../../docs/notes/separation-models.md "Separation models, weights and licences (separation agent, T020)"); DECISIONS separation entries]_
- Optional extra diffq is CC BY-NC 4.0: blocks any bundled or commercial release; soxr is LGPL-2.1 and accepted as a WARN. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer licence rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)")]_
- The confidence estimate (dominance times consistency) is uncalibrated and read 0.044 on the synthetic mix. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:19:39+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L26 "separate · - · synthetic-sep-v0 · wall_clock_s = 7.793 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)]_

- Calibrate the confidence estimate against measured quality
- Validate segmentation on real songs
- Try a RoFormer checkpoint if a licensed one is found

**Open blockers**

- No real-song stress set and the only guitar-capable RoFormer checkpoint has no stated licence; the stress curve so far is on a synthetic mix.

**Decisions**

- User-supplied stems accepted; hosted separation deferred ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- RoFormer opt-in and ungranted; default htdemucs_6s ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)"))
- GPU optional and not NVIDIA-specific ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))

**Risks**

- Separation artifacts mislead the loss: high (PLAN risk table)
- Several guitars in one stem: medium

#### Reference DI library (dry signal)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, real-songs

**Purpose.** Supply a clean DI clip that matches a tone's register and style, because the engines need dry input and the song has none.  
**Done when.** A licence-clean DI library is indexed locally, the picker returns clips that make the loss rank the true preset first more often than a single fixed clip does, and the user's own DI is a working accuracy mode.  
**Functionality today.** Indexes a folder of DI clips by register and style and picks one; the local Guitar-TECHS set is indexed and the picker chose the DI for the DS-Sample match. The user-DI play-along capture (reamp-capture) records the user's own guitar dry and through the pedal in one stream; a hardware take of it appears to exist (see discrepancies).  
**Progress note.** The indexer and picker exist and the Guitar-TECHS DI subset (104 clips, about 5 h) is downloaded, indexed and was used by the first real-song match. The play-along capture of the user's own DI is built and tested offline. Still unmeasured: whether the picker beats a fixed clip, and the finding that preset loss depends strongly on the DI (7.7 versus 11.0 and 22.7 on two other DIs).  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T021](../../board/TASKS.md#t021 "Reference DI library index over EGDB"), [T021b](../../board/TASKS.md#t021b "Download Guitar-TECHS DI subset into data/di/, index, real smoke clip"), [T042](../../board/TASKS.md#t042 "Play-along user-DI capture")  
**Owners.** dry-signal

**Implemented**

- index_library and pick_di over an EGDB-style library with a manifest, plus a synthetic fallback [`src/tonematch/dry.py`](../../src/tonematch/dry.py "Reference DI library: index a LOCAL folder of clean guitar DI audio, pick clips by register and style.") [`docs/notes/dry-signal.md`](../../docs/notes/dry-signal.md "Dry signal: reference DI library (option B), T021") `data/di/README.md`
- User-DI play-along capture support [`src/tonematch/user_di.py`](../../src/tonematch/user_di.py "Play-along user-DI capture on the HX Stomp (plan Step 7 dry signal, accuracy mode A; skill `reamp-capture`).") [`tests/test_user_di.py`](../../tests/test_user_di.py "Offline tests for the play-along user-DI capture (reamp-capture --user-di --play). Fake audio")

**Evidence**

- **unit-tests**: [tests/test_dry.py](../../tests/test_dry.py "tests/test_dry.py"); [tests/test_user_di.py](../../tests/test_user_di.py "Offline tests for the play-along user-DI capture (reamp-capture --user-di --play). Fake audio")
- **real-songs**: [scripts/match_song.py](../../scripts/match_song.py "End-to-end match of ONE song section on Helix Native (T041). LOCAL ONLY: no song audio leaves the machine.") used dry.pick_di on the DS-Sample target ([T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song")); no ledger row measures the picker itself

**Challenges and findings**

- DI mismatch biases EQ and gain; content-invariant losses and several reference DIs per tone are the mitigation, neither measured yet. _[PLAN risk table]_
- Licences of candidate DI datasets are not yet recorded in a model card or dataset card; licence-check flags data/ for hand review. _[reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md]_
- Preset loss is strongly DI-dependent: the same chain scores 7.7 on the picked DI and 11.0 and 22.7 on two others, so a fixed DI may bias the match. _[board T041; [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_

**Still to do**

- Measure whether the picker beats a fixed clip on the synthetic set
- Hardware-test the user-DI accuracy mode and report
- Blind DI recovery and A+B hybrid are research only

**Open blockers**

- The picker's benefit is unmeasured; the user-DI accuracy mode needs a hardware test with the human's guitar.

**Decisions**

- Dry signal v0: B (reference DI library) default, E (blind estimation) as starting guess ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))

**Risks**

- DI mismatch biases EQ and gain: medium (PLAN risk table)

#### Loss kit (tone metrics)

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy, synthetic

**Purpose.** Score how close a candidate render is to the target tone, with terms that are trustworthy despite separation artifacts and DI mismatch.  
**Done when.** At least one loss term, or a combination, passes the loss-trust-check threshold on the Helix synthetic set (true preset ranked first against distractors), so it has non-zero default weights, and the ear agrees with the metric on real songs.  
**Functionality today.** LossKit.score() returns per-term scores (LTAS tilt, crest/flatness/HNR, envelope, multi-resolution STFT) but with default weights 0 its objective is flat; the search must pass provisional weights explicitly and label results unadmitted. Update 2026-10-17: a second multi-resolution spectral term (mrstft_v2, version 2.0) is admitted alone, for the known-DI mode only, at weight 1.0; the default and provisional weights are unchanged.  
**Progress note.** Four no-DI terms and a LossKit exist, but none is admitted (all default weights 0) and the only trust measurement is on non-Helix proxy signals. Envelope v1 ([T025](../../board/TASKS.md#t025 "Sharpen envelope (weakest proxy term, held-out rank-1 0.05)")) improved the weakest term but held-out rank-1 on fresh seeds is only 0.117 against chance 0.0625 and 15 distractors; learned embeddings were researched, not run. AFx-Rep (ST-ITO) was wired in as an unadmitted embedding term and was worse than the DSP terms on proxy signals. On the first real song the loss ranked chain K first by 0.015 over H, while the human informally preferred H; the three level-invariant terms also accepted an output 52 dB too quiet until a guard was added in match_song.py. 2026-10-16: the first trust check on real Helix Native renders (helix-trust-v1: 40 known chains, 19 look-alikes each, 2,400 renders) admitted no term. On new notes of the same recording the best term managed rank-1 0.175 (7 of 40; chance 0.05), against a proposed bar of 0.90. With the same notes and the separation stand-in, mrstft reached 0.725 (0.875 against audible look-alikes only). The terms move more with the notes played than with the tone; reweighting did not help (0.15). No weights admitted (ledger [2026-10-16T15:50:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L597 "loss-trust · native · helix-trust-v1 · rank1_rate = 0.025")). [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B") (2026-10-17): mrstft_v2 2.0 was tuned on the train split, frozen, and scored once on the test split (0.990 against 0.90), admitted for the known-DI mode by the lead. It has not been judged by ear and has not been used on real songs.  
**Board tasks.** 6 of 6 done: [T022](../../board/TASKS.md#t022 "Loss kit v0 with the no-DI terms"), [T025](../../board/TASKS.md#t025 "Sharpen envelope (weakest proxy term, held-out rank-1 0.05)"), [T032](../../board/TASKS.md#t032 "Wire AFx-Rep (ST-ITO) in as loss term embed_afxrep"), [T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T067](../../board/TASKS.md#t067 "Gate 2 priority 1: loss-trust-check on real Helix Native renders"), [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B")  
**Owners.** tone-metrics

**Challenges and findings**

- No loss term is admitted. Against random distractors the provisional sum ranked the truth first 0.95 of the time, but against near distractors (one parameter group re-drawn) held-out rank-1 was 0.225 at best; easy distractors overstate the kit. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- mrstft is only valid with an aligned DI (0.775 same-notes, 0.00 held-out), so it cannot be used on a song with an unknown DI. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:27:57+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L33 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.225 (first of 8 rows at this time)") (8 rows)]_
- Envelope is the weakest term. v1 (spectral-flux onsets, sub-ms attack, per-note decay, sustain slope, noise floor) lifted held-out rank-1 from 0.033 to 0.117 on fresh seeds, still weak; same-notes 0.15 to 0.325 mostly reflects knowing the notes. Crest after the cab barely tracks drive; spectral flatness carries that term. _[board T025; ledger [2026-10-14T21:17:05](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L52 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.0333 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)..21:20:25+00:00]_
- The confidence mask had no effect on the proxy because the proxy separator introduced no artifacts; its value is untested. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 tone-metrics](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-tone-metrics-t032-afx-rep-as-embed_afxrep "2026-10-14 (tone-metrics, T032 AFx-Rep as embed_afxrep)") entry]_
- Only AFx-Rep is cleanly licensed (Apache-2.0, 1.16 GB checkpoint); Fx-Encoder++ is CC BY-NC 4.0 and Open-Amp has no licence file. No weights downloaded. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- AFx-Rep embedding term (embed_afxrep, weight 0): held-out rank-1 0.067 versus 0.133 for ltas_tilt and 0.150 for envelope on fresh proxy seeds, so worse and not admitted; 0.065 s per 10 s clip on CPU. _[board T032; ledger [2026-10-14T23:14:41](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L56 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.0667 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows) loss-trust]_
- The loss does not agree with the human on the one real song: it ranked chain K first by 0.015, the human preferred H. One song, informal, not blind, so this is a warning and not a measurement. _[ledger [2026-10-15T19:10:17](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0") human ab]_
- The loss terms are level-invariant, so a 52 dB quieter output scored better; match_song.py adds a level guard that is not an admitted term. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_

- Option C: note-proof terms for the no-DI case ([Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)"))

#### Block probe and fingerprints

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy

**Purpose.** Characterise each Helix block on its own (frequency response, harmonic and clipping curve, intermodulation, dynamics, delay, reverb and modulation) with a fixed probe signal, so search can choose models per slot from a fingerprint library and the Native-versus-Stomp audit can find mismatches like Early Reflections block by block.  
**Done when.** The probe signal is rendered through any single block at a grid of settings in Native (and optionally the Stomp), fingerprints are stored locally, compare tolerances are set from measured repeat spread and parity blocks, the per-block parity audit has run, and search uses the fingerprints to seed model choice.  
**Functionality today.** Builds the probe signal and layout, analyses a recorded response into a fingerprint and compares two fingerprints. Works on synthetic blocks only (non-Helix stand-ins); no Helix block has been probed.  
**Progress note.** The offline half is done and validated: the probe set, analysis and fingerprint format exist and 63 of 63 checks pass against known numpy and scipy blocks. Nothing has been run through Native or the Stomp, so no Helix block has a fingerprint, the compare tolerances are provisional, and the probe is 47 s against a roughly 30 s per model budget.  
**Board tasks.** 4 of 4 done: [T046](../../board/TASKS.md#t046 "block-probe skill, offline part"), [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)"), [T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order"), [T049](../../board/TASKS.md#t049 "Per-block parity audit on the Stomp using the probe set")  
**Owners.** tone-metrics, native-engine, benchmark

**Implemented**

- Probe signal, analysis, fingerprint schema (v1) and synthetic validation blocks [`src/tonematch/probe/signals.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/signals.py "The block-probe signal set, version `probe-v1` (T046).") [`src/tonematch/probe/analysis.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/analysis.py "Analyse a block's render of the probe set into a fingerprint (schema in docs/notes/block-probe.md).") [`src/tonematch/probe/fingerprint.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/fingerprint.py "Block fingerprint format (schema `tonematch.block-fingerprint` v1) and the per-feature compare.") [`src/tonematch/probe/synthetic.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/synthetic.py "Known synthetic blocks (numpy/scipy) for validating the block-probe analysis. NOT Helix models.")
- Command line: make, analyse, compare, validate [`scripts/block_probe.py`](../../scripts/block_probe.py "block-probe, offline part (owner: tone-metrics, T046). Skill: .claude/skills/block-probe/SKILL.md.") [`tests/test_probe.py`](../../tests/test_probe.py "block-probe offline part (T046): probe set, analysis against KNOWN synthetic blocks, fingerprint")

**Challenges and findings**

- Modulation is measured on a sustained carrier, not the impulse, because one impulse cannot show an LFO rate; the probe is 47 s (38 s without the DI excerpts), above the roughly 30 s per model assumed for the overnight capture, so a shorter variant may be needed. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 block-probe offline part](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-block-probe-offline-part-tone-metrics-t046 "2026-10-15 (block-probe offline part, tone-metrics, T046)")]_
- Compare tolerances are provisional until the Native repeat-render spread and a Stomp-versus-Native spread on blocks known to be in parity are measured. All validation so far is on non-Helix stand-ins and says nothing about how Helix blocks behave. _[[docs/notes/block-probe.md](../../docs/notes/block-probe.md "block-probe (T046, offline part)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:31:39](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L141 "smoke · - · probe-synth-v1 · probe_checks_pass_rate = 1.0")]_

**Decisions**

- Blocks are characterised individually, matches are judged on the whole chain ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 per-block approach, human-approved](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-per-block-approach-human-approved "2026-10-15 (per-block approach, human-approved)"))

#### Search and optimiser

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, hardware, real-songs, ear (informal, not blind)

**Purpose.** Find the preset whose render best matches the target section within a render budget set by the engine's cost.  
**Done when.** On the Helix synthetic-v1 set the search recovers known presets at the thresholds benchmark set (block-type F1, model accuracy, parameter MAE), then beats the nearest community preset on real songs, with the final candidate verified on the pedal.  
**Functionality today.** match() plans a render budget, searches a chain and its parameters with CMA-ES against an unadmitted loss, applies a level guard and a weak prior, and writes a report and listening pack. match_song.py ran it on one real song: best provisional loss 7.71 (raw DI 24.93). Renders now cost about 0.28 s, so earlier budgets were over-planned. Recovery of known answers is unproven on real engine renders. Current method: broad draws over 47 pool models, top-K narrowing, successive halving with banded CMA-ES within 300 renders, with measured seeding of delay, reverb and modulation settings.  
**Progress note.** CMA-ES over a captured template, beam over chains, budget planning, a level guard and a report writer are built, and one real-song run completed end to end on Native (449 renders, 1506 s; provisional loss 13.79 to 7.71, unadmitted). But the loss it optimises is not trusted (the human preferred the runner-up), recovery of known answers is shown only on a fake engine, there is no Helix known-answer run, no predictor or retrieval seeds, no model choice per slot, and no successive halving on hardware. The search now uses the Stomp speed options by default ([T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…"): 3 s search clips, readback every 10 loads, measured tails, one engine per batch; a legacy flag falls back), tested with a fake engine only. The trial time on the pedal was measured as a batch (10.9 s to 3.8 s, ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), but the search itself has not been timed on the pedal, so there is no ledger row for its speed. 2026-10-16: the clip-length study ([T065](../../board/TASKS.md#t065 "Clip-length study on Native")) kept the full-clip default (medians 8.36 full against 9.16-11.95 for shorter policies; ledger [2026-10-16T15:24:06+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L470 "search · native · ds-sample-intro-cliplen · final_score_median_full_15seeds = 8.49 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). [T074](../../board/TASKS.md#t074 "N1 free every numeric CC-mapped, verified param over its template…") (written, not committed, no render run): frees every CC-verified numeric parameter over its template range, with a range-scaled prior, and makes time-variant blocks opt-in. 2026-10-16/17: the known-DI level guard ([T096b](../../board/TASKS.md#t096b "Ruling 3: known-DI level guard references the target level")) is coded; model choice per slot ([T050](../../board/TASKS.md#t050 "Model choice per slot from the block library, fingerprint-informed…")) is built and, on a 16-item development set with the old provisional score, tied the old pool pass at 7 of 16 models (DECISIONS 2026-10-16; no ledger row). The re-run with the admitted score was interrupted. 2026-10-17: measured seeding ([T113](../../board/TASKS.md#t113 "Gate 2 step, raise single-block model accuracy")) and the hybrid method ([T114](../../board/TASKS.md#t114 "Gate 2 step: --hybrid"), chosen by a rule fixed before the last run) are built. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 items did not pass: it met four of six criteria and failed category accuracy (0.802 against 0.85) and model non-inferiority to the random walk. Weakest families: modulation, reverb, amp. The next attempt uses a fresh synthetic-v2 and per-family specialists. A chain builder ([T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")")) exists with fake engines only.  
**Board tasks.** 12 of 13 done: [T023](../../board/TASKS.md#t023 "CMA-ES over the fixed template using cost() for the budget"), [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song"), [T050](../../board/TASKS.md#t050 "Model choice per slot from the block library, fingerprint-informed…"), [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…"), [T065](../../board/TASKS.md#t065 "Clip-length study on Native"), [T074](../../board/TASKS.md#t074 "N1 free every numeric CC-mapped, verified param over its template…"), [T096b](../../board/TASKS.md#t096b "Ruling 3: known-DI level guard references the target level"), [T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")"), [T113](../../board/TASKS.md#t113 "Gate 2 step, raise single-block model accuracy"), [T114](../../board/TASKS.md#t114 "Gate 2 step: --hybrid"), [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist"), [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)"), [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear")

**Challenges and findings**

- With default LossKit weights of 0 the objective is flat, so search must be given PROVISIONAL_WEIGHTS and its output labelled unadmitted. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- Budget.clip_s is 2.0 s today while loss statistics need 10 s or more; the budget needs review. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- Native varies only 16 knobs and 16 switches inside a captured structure, so model choice cannot be searched on Native without new captures; model search is realistic only on the Stomp at about 2.7 s per render plus 0.66 s per model change. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- Plan.est_wall_s divided by cost().parallelism although match() renders serially (estimated 3.9 s, took 46 s) and popsize was rounded up to the parallelism for no gain. The planner now plans with parallelism 1 and notes the unused parallelism; a CMA-ES generation through NativeRenderPool or render_many would use the 2 renders/s pool but is not built. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 native-engine T026](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-native-engine-render-time-gap-t026 "2026-10-14 (native-engine, render-time gap, T026)"); [src/tonematch/search/loop.py](../../src/tonematch/search/loop.py "The matching loop: budget planning, CMA-ES refinement per chain, successive halving, chain grouping.")]_
- The first real-song run settled on corner values (several parameters at 1.0), and the human, listening informally through the Stomp outputs, judged the runner-up chain (rank 2, H) the best frequency match. The ear decides whether corners are a tone or a loss artefact; this is one song and not blind. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:10:17](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0")]_
- Without a level guard the search drove channel volume to the floor and cut the loss in half on a render that would be silent; the guard is not part of the admitted loss. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_
- Per-DI sensitivity: the same winning preset scores 7.7 on the picked DI and 11.0 and 22.7 on two others. _[board [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song")]_
- Scores before the Native host fix are history-dependent, so earlier run numbers are not exactly reproducible. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)")]_

**Still to do**

- Re-plan budgets at about 0.3 s per render
- Predictor and retrieval seeds (needs Learning)
- Successive halving on the Stomp and a hardware final check of the top candidate
- Make cab Early Reflections fixed at 0 for anything verified on the Stomp until parity exists
- Time the search with the Stomp speed options on the pedal
- Build per-family specialists: modulation, reverb and delay, amp (DECISIONS 2026-10-17)
- Run attempt 2 on synthetic-v2 under the frozen rule

**Open blockers**

- [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 failed ranking the true preset first (0.9375 against 0.95); modulation and drive are weak; attempt 3 needs a fresh sealed set.

**Decisions**

- Search passes provisional weights explicitly ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)"))
- Edit-buffer-only rule and cost() budgets ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1 and 2026-10-14 cycle](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)") 2)
- Bound free parameters and fix cab Early Reflections at 0 for Stomp-bound runs ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 parity finding](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-parity-finding-cab-earlyreflections-lead-on-benchmark "2026-10-15 (parity finding: cab EarlyReflections, lead on benchmark)"))

**Risks**

- Search space too large for the budget: medium (PLAN risk table)
- Hardware budget too small for a close match: medium

#### Benchmark, ledger and gates (evaluation)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, hardware

**Purpose.** Measure progress with known answers, record every run in an append-only ledger, and decide when a gate is passed.  
**Done when.** The synthetic-v1 recovery set is captured and rendered, thresholds are agreed, CI runs the smoke recovery on every change, the separation stress curve and parity null are recorded, and Gates 2 and 3 are evidenced and signed.  
**Functionality today.** Appends and reads the ledger, runs engine-smoke, generates 180 labelled synthetic items (labels, no audio), and measures Stomp-versus-Native parity on six presets with a restore check. Cannot yet render the known-answer set, run a recovery, or run a blind listen.  
**Progress note.** The ledger, [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)"), the synthetic-benchmark design and labels generator, and now a parity-null tool with a measured Stomp-versus-Native table exist. No Helix known-answer set has been rendered, no recovery has run, thresholds are approved as working targets but not frozen, no separation stress curve exists, there is no blind-ab script, and CI still skips the benchmark. 2026-10-16: parity spot checks on firmware 3.80 ([T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp")) and time-effect parity by measurement ([T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T066b](../../board/TASKS.md#t066b "Parity probe session closing the T066 open questions"), [T066c](../../board/TASKS.md#t066c "Parity method rule and the Gray Flanger / Deluxe Phaser close-out")) set where Native stands in for the pedal; the 96-item synthetic-v1 set and its runner are built ([T073](../../board/TASKS.md#t073 "Gate 2 synthetic recovery")), but only a 3-item provisional run exists (true preset first 0 of 3 on new notes, not gate evidence). [T100](../../board/TASKS.md#t100 "Ruling 2 in scripts/synthetic_v1.py") (Ruling 2, same-notes rank-1 in the known-DI mode) and the blind A/B tool ([T104](../../board/TASKS.md#t104 "Song and artist context as a prior"), benchmark) are done; Native verification ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")) is running. 2026-10-17: the pass rule was frozen before the full run; attempt 1 is recorded and [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") is not ticked. A blind A/B tool ([T107](../../board/TASKS.md#t107 "Blind A/B listening tool scripts/ab_session.py")) and a local explainer report ([T115](../../board/TASKS.md#t115 "Local Gate 2 report for a non-technical reader")) exist; the explainer stays off the public repository.  
**Board tasks.** 13 of 14 done, 1 in progress: [T008](../../board/TASKS.md#t008 "engine-smoke script and the first ledger row"), [T015](../../board/TASKS.md#t015 "Re-run engine-smoke on each engine once one is present"), [T019](../../board/TASKS.md#t019 "Own Stomp engine-smoke row once the human approves running the local…"), [T024](../../board/TASKS.md#t024 "Synthetic recovery set v1 and its thresholds"), [T040](../../board/TASKS.md#t040 "Parity null (human request)"), [T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp"), [T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T073](../../board/TASKS.md#t073 "Gate 2 synthetic recovery"), [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B"), [T100](../../board/TASKS.md#t100 "Ruling 2 in scripts/synthetic_v1.py"), [T104](../../board/TASKS.md#t104 "Song and artist context as a prior"), [T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py"), [T107](../../board/TASKS.md#t107 "Blind A/B listening tool scripts/ab_session.py"), [T115](../../board/TASKS.md#t115 "Local Gate 2 report for a non-technical reader")

**Challenges and findings**

- The plan's 'every change' synthetic run can only be a 6-item Native smoke recovery (about 7.5 min); the full Native pass is nightly (about 9.4 h) and Stomp recovery is milestone-only because hardware renders are serial. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- Native can only test which of three fixed structures and bypass switches and knobs, so model accuracy is meaningful only on the Stomp sub-set. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- A benchmark re-run of stompd from the scratchpad was blocked by the permission system the first time; a benchmark-owned row exists since 18:09:13. _[[board/GATES.md](../../board/GATES.md "Gates") [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") note]_
- Cab Early Reflections does not match between the Stomp (firmware 3.50) and Native 3.82: at 0 the search winner nulls at -31.4 dB, at its chosen 0.783 only -3.6 dB, worsening smoothly with the value. Every other parameter at its extreme stayed in parity. Cause untested. The hardware-parity claim holds for Early Reflections at 0 only. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 parity finding](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-parity-finding-cab-earlyreflections-lead-on-benchmark "2026-10-15 (parity finding: cab EarlyReflections, lead on benchmark)"); ledger [2026-10-15T02:48:51](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L108 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -31.35 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)]_
- Parity rows are -30 to -37 dB on clean and crunch presets and -19 dB on a delay preset; the cause of the delay case is not recorded. A void row (-6.41 dB) came from a stale Native template and is excluded. _[ledger [2026-10-15T00:25:36](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L96 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -30.96 (first of 9 rows at this time)") (9 rows); [ledger/void.csv](../../ledger/void.csv "ledger/void.csv")]_
- The ledger has parity, human ab and render-cost rows now, but still no benchmark, stress or engine-compare rows, so Gates 2 and 3 have no evidence. _[[ledger/runs.csv](../../ledger/runs.csv "ledger/runs.csv")]_

#### Learning (predictor, proxies)

**Status:** not-started · **Health:** unknown · **Evidence:** none

**Purpose.** Learn from rendered pairs to seed the search with a blind predictor and, later, to speed the inner loop with neural block proxies and a learned similarity metric.  
**Done when.** A predictor trained on Helix-rendered pairs improves the search's start (measurably fewer renders to the same loss), its weights have a model card and a licence that allows publishing, and the flywheel adds verified matches as training data.  
**Functionality today.** Nothing. There is no data generator, predictor or proxy.  
**Progress note.** No code, data or model; only the task [T030](../../board/TASKS.md#t030 "Data generator: EGDB DI clips through random plausible chains on…") exists and the plan defers neural proxies until after week 6.  
**Board tasks.** 0 of 1 done: [T030](../../board/TASKS.md#t030 "Data generator: EGDB DI clips through random plausible chains on…")  
**Owners.** learning

**Implemented**

_Nothing implemented yet._

**Evidence**

- **none**: no evidence recorded

- The Native EULA is silent on training ML on Native renders; prefer hardware renders for anything published until the installer EULAs are read. _[[docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md](../../docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md "Line 6 EULA flags for automation and protocol use (not legal advice)"); PLAN risk table]_

#### Blind listening sessions and export

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** ear (informal, not blind)

**Purpose.** Prepare level-matched, randomised blind A/B or MUSHRA sessions so the human, who is the final judge, can rate matches against references and baselines.  
**Done when.** A blind-ab-session script builds a session folder with the answer key kept out, the human has rated the 20-song set, results are ingested as ledger rows, and [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") has the human's sign-off.  
**Functionality today.** No blind session can be prepared yet. Informal listening happened outside the tool: the human compared the target stem, two matches, the start chain and the raw DI for one section of one song, and played one preset live on the Stomp. **Progress note.** Two informal human listening sessions exist as ledger rows (not blind, one song, not scored by any agent). - The ear is the final judge and no preset has ever been listened to against a reference; every quality claim in this report is metric or hardware-level only. _[CLAUDE.md standing rules]_

#### End-to-end song-to-preset pipeline and CLI

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, real-songs, ear (informal, not blind)

**Purpose.** Give it a song, get back an HX Stomp .hlx with up to three snapshots, verified on the pedal.  
**Done when.** One command takes a song (and optional stems), runs separation, segmentation, DI choice, search and export, and returns a validated .hlx whose result is verified on the engine of the user's choice.  
**Functionality today.** [scripts/match_song.py](../../scripts/match_song.py "End-to-end match of ONE song section on Helix Native (T041). LOCAL ONLY: no song audio leaves the machine.") takes one prepared song section to an exported .hlx and a listening pack on one machine; the human then loaded the preset on the pedal and listened. Not a product command: one song (DS-Sample), unadmitted loss, hand-run steps, and nothing for the other sections.  
**Progress note.** A script runs the chain on a real song locally (separation cache, section choice, DI pick, search on Native, .hlx export); one song has been taken through it and the result was loaded onto the pedal by hand with the whole-preset injection tool. There is still no CLI entry point, no pipeline module, no automatic Stomp verification step, no snapshot assignment from section groups and only one song.  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song")  
**Owners.** tone-matcher-lead, search

**Implemented**

- Plan and README [`docs/PLAN.md`](../../docs/PLAN.md "HX Stomp Tone Matcher — Project Plan") [`README.md`](../../README.md "HX Stomp Tone Matcher")
- Local end-to-end script and its tests (fake engine) [`scripts/match_song.py`](../../scripts/match_song.py "End-to-end match of ONE song section on Helix Native (T041). LOCAL ONLY: no song audio leaves the machine.") [`tests/test_match_song.py`](../../tests/test_match_song.py "scripts/match_song.py plumbing on a fake engine and synthetic stems (no Native, no song audio).")

**Evidence**

- **unit-tests**: [tests/test_match_song.py](../../tests/test_match_song.py "scripts/match_song.py plumbing on a fake engine and synthetic stems (no Native, no song audio).")
- **real-songs**: board [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song"): one real song run end to end locally (449 Native renders, 1506 s); the loss is unadmitted
- **ear (informal, not blind)**: ledger [2026-10-15T19:10:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0") and [19:20:34](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L133 "ab · stomp · DS-Sample · live_pref_delay_mix = 0.1")+00:00: informal, one song, not blind

**Challenges and findings**

- The first end-to-end run found four integration problems at once: a level-blind loss, an ignored exact 0.0 command, host memory of 5 to 11 GB with two kills, and a strong DI dependence of the loss. The first two memory and 0.0 findings are fixed in the engine; the loss problems are not. _[board T041; [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)"), 2026-10-15 host memory]_
- The song audio and stems are local only (gitignored cache); this page and the repository carry no audio. The song is referred to by its file id DS-Sample only. _[CLAUDE.md standing rules]_

**Open blockers**

- Depends on the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") verification and sign-off, the chain builder (not built), a score that works without a clean recording, and automatic hardware verification.

**Risks**

- Several guitars in one stem: medium
- Studio processing on the record: medium

#### Platform, install and packaging

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Install and run on Windows, macOS and Linux with whichever GPU backend is present, and ship releases others can use.  
**Done when.** A clean install works on all three OSes in CI, GPU backends are packaged, Native on Linux works through Wine and yabridge or is documented as unsupported, and a release package exists.  
**Functionality today.** pip install -e .\[dev\] works on this Mac; detect_backend picks cpu or mps; CI runs ruff and pytest on Ubuntu with Python 3.11 only.  
**Progress note.** Dev-environment notes, a GPU backend detector and a Linux-only CI for tests and lint exist; the backend is verified only as CPU and MPS on one arm64 Mac. No Windows or Linux run, no packaging, and the pyproject has no entry points. The project licence is now MIT. 2026-10-17: elastic job runner ([T138](../../board/TASKS.md#t138 "Elastic jobs for the host scaler")) and Native host cap raised to 8.  
**Board tasks.** 2 of 2 done: [T007](../../board/TASKS.md#t007 "Dev environment notes for Windows, macOS, Linux"), [T138](../../board/TASKS.md#t138 "Elastic jobs for the host scaler")

**Challenges and findings**

- The CI runs on Ubuntu without the optional extras, so the engine, separation and Native paths are untested there; python-rtmidi has no wheel on macOS with Python 3.14. _[.github/workflows/ci.yml; pyproject.toml comments]_
- The project licence is not chosen; pedalboard (GPLv3) and non-commercial diffq constrain what can be bundled. _[pyproject.toml; reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md]_

**Still to do**

- Entry points and installers
- GPU packaging per backend

#### Licensing and legal hygiene

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Keep Line 6 assets out of the repo, respect the GPLv3 boundary and dependency licences, and flag EULA and model-weight questions before they bite.  
**Done when.** licence-check passes on every dependency change with every WARN justified, the EULAs have been read, the project licence is chosen, and no release ships a non-commercial or unlicensed component.  
**Functionality today.** licence_check.py scans dependency licences, the pedalboard import boundary, Line 6 assets and committed golden presets and writes a review; it passes today. The project is MIT; third-party components keep their own licences.  
**Progress note.** licence_check.py runs and passes with documented WARN rulings, the pedalboard boundary is enforced by a test, no Line 6 asset is in the repo, and the project licence is MIT (LICENSE present, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES updated; the copyright line is derived from the GitHub name and awaits the human's confirmation). Not done: the human marked the installer EULAs as read but recorded no finding, diffq is non-commercial (private research only), the guitar-capable RoFormer checkpoint has no stated licence, and the WARN dependencies need re-review before any bundled release.  
**Board tasks.** 6 of 6 done: [T009](../../board/TASKS.md#t009 "Pull the Native Pilot's Guide automation details and fretwire's…"), [T011](../../board/TASKS.md#t011 "First licence-check run over pyproject.toml once the engine spikes…"), [T016](../../board/TASKS.md#t016 "Fix the DeprecationWarning in scripts/licence_check.py"), [T016a](../../board/TASKS.md#t016a "Remaining from T016: DeprecationWarning at scripts/licence_check.py:47"), [T017](../../board/TASKS.md#t017 "Read the EULA shipped with the Native and HX Edit installers"), [T020b](../../board/TASKS.md#t020b "RoFormer adapter licence read + install")  
**Owners.** reviewer, research

**Implemented**

- licence_check.py, allowlist with DECISIONS references, review reports [`scripts/licence_check.py`](../../scripts/licence_check.py "licence-check (owner: reviewer). See .claude/skills/licence-check/SKILL.md.") [`docs/licence-allowlist.json`](../../docs/licence-allowlist.json "docs/licence-allowlist.json") [`reviews/licence-2026-10-13.md`](../../reviews/licence-2026-10-13.md "licence-check 2026-10-13") [`reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md`](../../reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md "licence-check 2026-10-14")

**Challenges and findings**

A WARN is not a clearance: soxr, lameenc, tqdm, certifi need re-review before any bundled release. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)"); board [T016a](../../board/TASKS.md#t016a "Remaining from T016: DeprecationWarning at scripts/licence_check.py:47")]_
- The generic Line 6 EULA forbids reverse engineering beyond interoperability and hosted or cloud use, and is silent on ML training from Native renders. This is not legal advice. _[[docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md](../../docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md "Line 6 EULA flags for automation and protocol use (not legal advice)")]_

**Decisions**

- No firmware decompilation ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- No Line 6 data or binaries in the repo (same entry)
- soxr, lameenc, tqdm, certifi accepted as WARN; diffq private research only ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 licence rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)"))
- Project licence MIT, approved by the human ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 project licence and human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-project-licence-lead-at-the-humans-request "2026-10-14 (project licence, lead, at the human's request)") approvals)

**Risks**

- Native EULA silent on ML training: the human's reading is not recorded in the repo (PLAN risk table)
- Public repository: no song audio, stems, Line 6 catalog content or firmware may be committed; golden .hlx and cache/ stay gitignored

#### Block-presence detector

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic

**Purpose.** Estimate which kinds of block (cabinet, gain stage, delay, reverb, modulation, compressor, equaliser) a target tone contains, to seed the chain builder.  
**Done when.** Passes the registered per-category bars on a held-out split and works behind an amp and cabinet.  
**Functionality today.** Returns per-category probabilities from a target and its clean recording (known-DI mode) or without it (no-DI mode).  
**Progress note.** Registered before scoring (presence-v1), scored once on the test split: only the cabinet (0.89 precision, 0.95 recall) and the gain-index ordering (0.77) pass; drive-or-amp, delay, reverb, modulation, compressor and equaliser miss their bars. Without the clean recording only "is there a gain stage" is usable. Accepted as a soft prior only.  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T102](../../board/TASKS.md#t102 "Block-presence detector for the chain builder")  
**Owners.** none

**Implemented**

- Presence features, model and evaluation script [`src/tonematch/metrics/presence.py`](../../src/tonematch/metrics/presence.py "Block-presence detector (T102): which effect categories a target guitar recording contains.") [`scripts/presence_eval.py`](../../scripts/presence_eval.py "Block-presence evaluation `presence-v1` (owner: tone-metrics, T102). Offline: no Native, no pedal.") [`tests/test_presence.py`](../../tests/test_presence.py "Block-presence detector (T102): measurements on synthetic signals, and invariance to the notes played.")

#### Chain builder

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Turn the analysis (blocks present, common patterns, per-slot model shortlists) into a whole chain and a preset file.  
**Done when.** Runs on a real engine and beats the nearest community preset on real songs ([Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)")).  
**Functionality today.** Assembles candidate chains using block-serial composition of single-block templates.  
**Progress note.** Built ([T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")")) with fake engines only: presence, top structures from the chain prior, per-slot shortlist, coordinate descent and joint refine, halving across structures. A real run needs a block-library adapter, block-serial rendering in the render pool and the presence estimate keys.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")"), [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder"), [T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")  
**Owners.** none

**Implemented**

- Chain builder and design note [`src/tonematch/search/chain_builder.py`](../../src/tonematch/search/chain_builder.py "Chain builder (T105): build a chain from the analysis, one block at a time.") [`docs/notes/chain-builder.md`](../../docs/notes/chain-builder.md "Chain builder (T105\"))") [`tests/test_chain_builder.py`](../../tests/test_chain_builder.py "Chain builder (T105) on fakes: fake block library, fake engine, fake loss, fake structures.")

- **unit-tests**: [tests/test_chain_builder.py](../../tests/test_chain_builder.py "Chain builder (T105) on fakes: fake block library, fake engine, fake loss, fake structures.") (13, fake engines)

#### Cabinet estimation by deconvolution

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy

**Purpose.** Compute a cabinet response from a target and its clean recording instead of searching for it.  
**Done when.** Picks the matching cabinet on real clips with the right amp, and works with an estimated amp.  
**Functionality today.** Computes a cabinet response in about 30 ms and matches it to the nearest cabinet model or library IR.  
**Progress note.** Analysis only ([T106](../../board/TASKS.md#t106 "IR library analysis (human 2026-10-17)")): with the right amp the cabinet model ranks first in 22 of 24 real-clip cases and an IR is recovered exactly 94 percent of the time; with a wrong amp model it fails (2 percent). IR audio and per-file data stay local; the library may be used for training (human, 2026-10-17).  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T106](../../board/TASKS.md#t106 "IR library analysis (human 2026-10-17)")  
**Owners.** none

**Implemented**

- Cabinet response, IR library analysis [`src/tonematch/metrics/cab_response.py`](../../src/tonematch/metrics/cab_response.py "Cab and impulse-response (IR) frequency responses: measure, describe, compare, estimate.") [`scripts/ir_library_analyze.py`](../../scripts/ir_library_analyze.py "Offline analysis of the user's impulse-response (IR) library against the Helix cab models (T106).") [`docs/notes/ir-library.md`](../../docs/notes/ir-library.md "User IR library: what it can do for the project (T106)")

#### Per-family specialists

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** synthetic

**Purpose.** One method per effect family (gain, linear, dynamics, time-based, modulation) with its own measurements, search and budget share.  
**Done when.** Each specialist beats the general method on its family at the same budget, judged by a paired comparison, and [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") passes on a fresh set.  
**Functionality today.** --specialists all routes each family to its specialist and refiner.  
**Progress note.** Five families have a specialist plugged in through a dispatcher ([T117](../../board/TASKS.md#t117 "Linear specialist for cab and EQ") linear, [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist") time-based, mod, amp, gain; [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)") and [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear") adapters). Each was adopted only after beating the general method on development items. In [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on sealed synthetic-v2 the combined method raised category to 0.865 and model to 0.760, but modulation (model 4 of 12) and drive (6 of 12) remain weak and the gate failed on ranking the true preset first (0.9375). Dynamics is a stub. Synthetic evidence only; no ear.  
**Board tasks.** 4 of 4 done: [T117](../../board/TASKS.md#t117 "Linear specialist for cab and EQ"), [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist"), [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)"), [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear")  
**Owners.** search, tone-metrics

**Implemented**

_Nothing implemented yet._

### Open items for the lead (agent-owned, not human actions)

- Rule on adding the ledger kind 'probe'
- Rule on the open Native proposals: clamp or handle an exact 0.0 command, 3 s pre-roll default for delay or reverb chains, template key with the bypass pattern
- Rule on the proposals open in DECISIONS (Native 0.0 handling, snapshot sync into the preset writer, loudness term, probe ledger kind)

### Discrepancies between sources

- [T018](../../board/TASKS.md#t018 "Spike: emit .hlx with knob/switch assignments pre-written so a new…") (the original 'spike awaiting go') is still an open line on the board but was superseded by [T018n](../../board/TASKS.md#t018n "Hands-off Native loading") (Native, done) and [T018s](../../board/TASKS.md#t018s "Spike: autonomous whole-preset injection on the HX Stomp") (Stomp, in progress): its scope is built, so the line is stale. Registry feature task lists use [T018n](../../board/TASKS.md#t018n "Hands-off Native loading") and [T018s](../../board/TASKS.md#t018s "Spike: autonomous whole-preset injection on the HX Stomp").
- [T044](../../board/TASKS.md#t044 "Native host memory: find and fix the growth") is marked done 'as [T043](../../board/TASKS.md#t043 "Native host memory (human report")' and [T043](../../board/TASKS.md#t043 "Native host memory (human report") is also marked done; both describe the same Native memory fix. The registry counts [T043](../../board/TASKS.md#t043 "Native host memory (human report") and [T044](../../board/TASKS.md#t044 "Native host memory: find and fix the growth") once each, so the Native task count is inflated by one.
- [T040](../../board/TASKS.md#t040 "Parity null (human request)") (parity null) is in progress on the board with the note 'blocked by native-engine autocapture report, lead go', but parity-v0 rows from six presets and the Early Reflections bisect exist in the ledger since [2026-10-15T00:25:36](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L96 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -30.96 (first of 9 rows at this time)") (9 rows); the board line is behind the ledger. The [2026-10-15T03:18:03](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L117 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -6.41 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows) parity rows are voided (stale Native template) in [ledger/void.csv](../../ledger/void.csv "ledger/void.csv") and are not counted.
- [T042](../../board/TASKS.md#t042 "Play-along user-DI capture") (play-along user-DI capture) is marked 'OFFLINE, hardware run pending', yet the [2026-10-15T19:10:17](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0") human listening row names 'user take6' of the DS-Sample section, so at least six takes were recorded on hardware. The board and ledger should say which commands were run on the pedal.
- [T022](../../board/TASKS.md#t022 "Loss kit v0 with the no-DI terms") and [T023](../../board/TASKS.md#t023 "CMA-ES over the fixed template using cost() for the budget") are marked done but mean 'built', not 'trusted': no loss term is admitted and the search has never run on a trusted loss or on the Helix known-answer set. [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song") added a real-song run with an unadmitted loss, which is not a benchmark row. [T024](../../board/TASKS.md#t024 "Synthetic recovery set v1 and its thresholds") is partly done: the 'every change (CI)' synthetic run cannot run until the set is rendered and .github/workflows/ci.yml still has the benchmark step commented out.
- [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)")'s Stomp tick was written on stomp-engine's rows; benchmark's own re-run ([2026-10-14T18:09:13](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L24 "smoke · stomp · smoke · peak_dbfs = -13.47 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)) corroborates it, but hx_edit_import_ok and pedal_load_ok are human-reported and not observed by benchmark.
- The stompd helper source is intentionally not in the repo (human decision, [docs/notes/stompd.md](../../docs/notes/stompd.md "stompd: the HX Stomp control helper (stomp-engine, 2026-10-14)")): Stomp ledger rows cannot be reproduced by others until a wrapper is published or re-written to the documented protocol. Recorded as a reproducibility gap, not a board error.
- Ledger hygiene: native-engine deleted one accidental row with sed on 2026-10-15 instead of appending a correction (recorded in [docs/DECISIONS.md](../../docs/DECISIONS.md "Decision log")); git shows only appended rows against the last commit, and the rule was restated.
- [T091](../../board/TASKS.md#t091 "S1 engine push: StompEngine.render_many") (two renders per Stomp pass) and [T095](../../board/TASKS.md#t095 "Engine-push pedal session") (engine-push pedal session) are partial on the board: the audio comparison of two-chain versus serial passes did not run because CoreAudio failed (ledger [2026-10-16T18:30:48+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L688 "smoke · stomp · smoke · restore_bytes_equal = 1.0 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). ## Glossary

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| blind A/B | A listening test where the listener does not know which option is which. |
| block | One element in a preset's chain: an amp, cabinet, drive pedal, delay and so on. |
| block library | A stored set of single-block Native setups, one per pedal effect model, used to build and render candidate presets quickly. |
| bootstrap interval | A range for a measured difference, built by re-sampling the 96 items many times. If the whole range sits on the good side of a line, the difference is reliable at the stated confidence. |
| capture | The one-off step that teaches Helix Native a new chain: the preset file is loaded into its editor and the resulting state is recorded. |
| chain builder | The planned stage that decides which kinds of block a tone needs, orders them by common patterns learned from real presets, and then fills each slot using the single-block methods. |
| CMA-ES | An optimisation algorithm used to search for suitable settings. |
| CoreAudio | The Mac's audio system. When it fails, no sound can be sent to or recorded from the pedal. |
| dB | Decibel, a logarithmic loudness or level-difference scale. |
| DI | Direct Input: a clean, unprocessed guitar recording, before any amp or effects. |
| DSP | Digital Signal Processing: the audio calculations inside amps and effects. |
| Early Reflections | A cabinet setting that adds the first short echoes of the room. |
| effect family | A group of effects that behave alike: gain (drive, fuzz, amp), linear (cabinet, equaliser), dynamics (compressor, gate), time-based (delay, reverb), modulation (chorus, flanger, phaser, tremolo). |
| EULA | The software licence agreement shown when installing a product. |
| firmware | The software running inside the pedal. |
| fw | Short for firmware, the pedal's built-in software. The pedal was on version 3.50 until 2026-10-15 and is now on 3.80. |
| gate | A milestone with a written checklist and a human sign-off. |
| golden file | A reference preset file the owner exported from HX Edit or Native, used to check that our own files follow the same layout. |
| held-out | Test items not used when tuning, so the result is not flattered by tuning. |
| Helix Native | Line 6's software version of the Helix sounds; it runs as a plugin and renders audio offline. |
| Hum and Ripple | Two amp settings that add a mains-hum style noise. The noise runs freely, so it differs between renders of the same preset. |
| HX Edit | Line 6's editing program for the pedal; it also imports preset files. |
| HX Stomp | The Line 6 guitar-effects pedal this project targets. |
| impulse response (IR) | A short recording of a speaker cabinet and microphone that can reproduce that cabinet's sound by convolution. A cabinet block in the pedal uses one. |
| known-answer set | A test set made by rendering presets we already know through Helix, so we can check whether the tool recovers them. |
| ledger | The project's append-only record of measured runs; a claim without a row did not happen. |
| look-alike | A candidate preset that differs slightly from the true one (a nearby setting or a neighbouring model); used to test whether a measure can tell near matches apart. |
| loss | The score the optimiser tries to lower: how different two sounds are. Lower is a closer match. |
| middle baseline | A baseline that sets every knob to the middle of its range and picks nothing cleverly. It shows what a do-nothing guess scores. |
| MIDI CC | A control message that moves one knob or switch from software. |
| null residual | What is left when one recording is subtracted from another that should match. Reported in decibels below the signal; the more negative, the closer. |
| numeric mode | Helix Native starts each session in one of two slightly different rounding modes. The difference is tiny (rounding level) but can break exact cancellation tests. |
| parity | Agreement between the two engines: the same preset is rendered on both and the recordings are compared. |
| Path A and Path B | The two signal paths of the HX Stomp. Using both at once renders two chains in one pass. |
| pre-roll | Extra audio played before the part that is recorded, so delays and reverbs start from the same state every time. |
| preset | A saved sound: a chain of amp, cabinet and effect blocks with their settings (an .hlx file). |
| random walk | A baseline search that tries 300 random settings and keeps the best, judged by the same trusted score. It scores high here because the tool has only 300 tries over a pool of 47 effect models and the score is a good judge, so even random guessing often lands on the right model. Earlier notes called it random search. |
| rank-1 rate | How often the true preset is ranked first among the candidates (the look-alikes). Higher is better; guessing among 20 gives 0.05. |
| readback | Reading the pedal's settings back after writing them to confirm they match. |
| render | Play a test signal through a preset and record the audio that comes out. |
| render pool | Several copies of Helix Native running at once, each rendering a different candidate, to finish a batch sooner. |
| search test | One candidate setting tried by the search: it is rendered and then scored against the target. |
| sentinel | A check run inside a long job that renders a fixed test now and then and stops the job if the result has drifted, so a corrupted render cannot be scored as real. |
| separation | Splitting a full mix into instruments so the guitar can be analysed alone. |
| session close | The last message a control program sends to the pedal when it is done. Without it the pedal stays in editing mode and its blocks cannot be edited by hand. |
| snapshot | One of up to three stored variations of a preset that the pedal can switch between. |
| specialist | A separate method for one family of effects (gain, linear, dynamics, time-based, modulation) with its own measurements and search, instead of one general method for everything. |
| stand-in signals | Test audio made by ordinary software effects instead of Helix. Useful for early checks; not proof the method works on Helix. |
| synthetic-v2 | A fresh known-answer set, made with the same generator and the same frozen rules but a new random seed, because the first set has now been seen. |
| time-variant block | An effect that changes its sound on its own over time (for example a free-running chorus), so two renders never cancel exactly. |
| tone comparison | The loss: the combined measures of how similar two guitar sounds are. |
| trust check | A test of whether a measure puts the true preset first among many candidates. |
| USB slip | A one-sample shift or short burst in the audio coming back from the pedal over its USB connection, which makes a render differ slightly from an identical earlier one. |
| wall-clock | Real elapsed time on a clock, as opposed to computing time. |
